3按目标选择的学习路线

先分清两条业务路径

观看路径 · 身份不等于权限钱包签名SIWE / nonce→会话与规则JWT / 数据库→持仓证据safe 区块 / 数量→播放能力内容 / 版本 / TTL→HLS 字节playlist / segment
内容路径 · 任务成功不等于媒体可播原件封存→DB / outbox→队列 / worker→generation 发布→解码与实播

链上登记是另一条持久化 intent → nonce → 签名事实 → 广播/恢复路径，不把视频字节写到链上。

按目标选择路线

准备面试

每次学习完成一张图、一段可验证复述或一份小实验。勾选代表自评，不代表已经通过业务验收。

证据边界：单元01～12及原阅读库依据快照 4bc3fab1，引用 d0f05152 的历史验收。单元13及新增四份源码固定到 c38dc4bc0bd5ac694261ee60ca07764783b1d05b（四份源码与当前候选 d267ad4439ba672fecf373cae22f07852a0f6ada 逐字节相同）；该扩展同候选默认CI run41823377757 attempt4已55/55成功、完整all原始退出0；后续候选 d267ad44 的默认CI run42548083086 attempt2 全绿，并留有公开 Sepolia 部分实证（六笔真实交易、链上oracle、真实SIWE登录与拒签）；完整TN矩阵与媒体播放证据仍待完成，公开验收未关闭。新文档或新合并 SHA 不继承旧 CI。Anvil、Mac 固定预算测量与生产/testnet 分开；不承诺 DRM、生产容量或本人已完成讲述。源文件旧日期、旧行号与示例仍需按当前代码核对。

一张分层图，八个数据问题

代码阅读方向

cmd/ 启动 → pkg/gateway/ 适配与装配 → pkg/middleware/ 身份/授权 → pkg/service/ 决策与状态推进 → pkg/storage/、pkg/web3/ 外部依赖。

单体优先，按需要拆分

优先在单体理解业务；再验证网关、独立 worker 和其他进程的实际通信。模块名不自动证明微服务隔离，部署拓扑不自动证明水平扩容能力。

01
全貌 / 业务边界

先把系统画出来，再打开代码

先回答三个问题：谁在证明什么、谁有权放行、视频字节从哪里来。

0 / 3

一条链上读路径，一条链下媒体路径

StreamGate 是中心化 Go 媒体服务，使用链上 NFT 持仓作为访问资格。钱包签名证明调用者控制账户；NFT 读取证明该账户在指定链、指定区块上满足规则；服务器签发短期播放能力，再从 MinIO/S3 交付 HLS。视频没有上传到链上，NFT 也不自动证明版权。

先区分四个权威

规则与任务状态由 PostgreSQL 决定；持仓由受约束的 RPC 观察提供；会话/共享控制状态按部署 profile 使用 Redis 等权威存储；媒体字节由对象存储保存。 缓存和消息队列不能替代这些权威。HTTP 处理器只负责适配，业务判断应追到 service 与中间件。

双模式是部署选择

从 cmd/monolith/streamgate/main.go 进入，跟到 gateway.SetupRouter、依赖装配和路由。再对照 api-gateway 的入口：有独立二进制不等于每次请求都跨微服务转发；实际调用关系必须看 providers 和部署配置。把职责、进程、容器三个概念分开，才不会把单体内部调用误画成 RPC。

本单元输出： 画一张包含用户、网关、规则库、链、对象存储和 worker 的图，每条箭头写“请求/凭据/字节/任务”中的一种。

动手前先预测结果

不运行容器。用两分钟说明：用户已经登录，为什么还可能无法播放？并在代码里找到至少两种不同的拒绝原因。

展开：源码定位与练习命令

命令从仓库根执行。未注释的容器命令仅做观察；真实业务练习需要已准备的专用环境。

git rev-parse HEAD
rg -n 'SetupRouter|provideAuthService|streamGroup.Use' pkg/gateway
rg -n 'published_generation|lease_owner' pkg/service/transcoding

参考复述与检查重点

登录只解决身份；访问还受服务器规则、持仓证据、内容状态和能力有效性约束。链上部分提供可转移资格的观察依据，平台仍负责规则、存储与交付。

02
Go / 生命周期 / 并发

把 C++／音视频经验迁移成 Go 判断力

把 goroutine 当作需要管理生命周期的工作，而不是免费的后台线程。

0 / 3

对照经验，理解不同的默认行为

C++ 的 RAII 与 Go 的 defer 都帮助收尾，但 defer 不是自动的跨资源事务。接口有动态类型和值两部分：一个携带 nil 指针的接口可能不等于 nil。路由装配专门处理 typed-nil Redis 客户端，避免把它误认为可用依赖。slice、map 和指针的复制也不等于深拷贝。

取消必须贯穿整个工作链

跟踪 context 从入口如何到数据库、RPC、worker 和子进程。关闭服务时，要停止新任务、取消或完成在途工作、等待 goroutine，再释放连接。仅停止循环不能证明 FFmpeg 及其子进程已经退出；仅加超时也不能证明所有阻塞调用都响应取消。

内存锁与数据库租约解决不同问题

mutex 约束同进程共享状态；数据库 lease 与 generation 约束跨进程任务所有权。taskSnapshot 会复制 metadata 及时间指针，避免把内部可变状态直接交给调用者。singleflight 合并同一键的在途工作，但不会自动保证证据新鲜、请求隔离或错误处理正确。

读代码时反复问： 谁创建、谁关闭、谁等待、谁拥有最终写入权？Go 风格之外，真正的能力是能指出一条泄漏路径或竞态窗口。

动手前先预测结果

找一个返回 map 或指针的读取函数，解释浅拷贝的风险；再画出一次 worker 停止时 context、租约和子进程各自发生什么。

展开：源码定位与练习命令

命令从仓库根执行。未注释的容器命令仅做观察；真实业务练习需要已准备的专用环境。

rg -n 'context.WithCancel|WaitGroup|Shutdown|Stop' pkg/core pkg/service/transcoding
rg -n 'taskSnapshot|metadataSnapshot|singleflight' pkg/service pkg/middleware

先合上参考答案，再自评
参考复述与检查重点

不要把并发正确性归结为“用了锁”。进程内共享状态、跨进程任务竞争、外部子进程资源和链上副作用，各自需要明确的所有权与退出协议。

03
SIWE / EIP-191 / 重放

钱包登录：签名、域绑定与一次性挑战

签名有效只是起点；消息内容、上下文和消费原子性同样决定安全。

0 / 3

从服务器生成 challenge 开始

GenerateWalletChallenge 规范化地址，生成 nonce，绑定地址、链、签名类型、签发及过期时间。默认 SIWE 是带有结构化字段的登录消息；通过 personal_sign 所用的 EIP-191 消息签名格式验证，不是发送一笔链上交易。未知签名类型会被拒绝，不能静默退化成另一种方式。

按真实代码顺序画图

AuthenticateWithWallet 先读取 challenge 并检查匹配、有效期及消费状态，再验证签名与 SIWE 字段，随后执行原子 MarkChallengeUsed，成功后才签发登录 token。早期描述中“先消费再验签”不能代替当前实现。并发请求可以同时通过初步检查，但只有原子消费的胜者能够获得 token。

登录 token 与播放能力不是同一种凭据

登录 JWT 表示服务会话；播放 token 绑定内容与授权上下文。签名校验之外还要检查 token 类型、audience、过期、会话及 epoch 等信息。开发代签与测试 mint 也不能被解释为产品的生产钱包方案。

能力边界： EVM 普通账户和 Solana 的签名路由可在代码中追踪，但不能由此宣称所有链均端到端验收。基础快照中的 ERC-1271 仍是 helper；增量候选 c38dc4bc 已接入账户分类、合约验签和有界会话，见单元13。代码已实现不等于公开路径已验收。

动手前先预测结果

在纸上并列画出两个重放同一签名的请求，标出最终竞争点。再解释：拒绝一个错误域的签名，为何不同于验证 ECDSA 数学正确性？

展开：源码定位与练习命令

命令从仓库根执行。未注释的容器命令仅做观察；真实业务练习需要已准备的专用环境。

rg -n 'GenerateWalletChallenge|AuthenticateWithWallet|MarkChallengeUsed' pkg/service/auth_wallet.go
rg -n 'EVAL|script|MarkChallengeUsed' pkg/storage/redis_challenge.go
rg -n 'SetEIP1271Checker|NewMultiChainSignatureVerifier' pkg

先合上参考答案，再自评
参考复述与检查重点

消息必须属于本服务、本链、本挑战和本账户；原子消费必须位于 token 签发之前。签名是必要证据，授权和会话策略仍由服务端执行。

04
ERC-721/1155 / 安全缓存

NFT 门禁：数量、规则与同区块证据

证明的是一个具体命题，不是一个可永久复用的 hasNFT 布尔值。

0 / 3

先确定“查什么”来自谁

规则指定 chain、contract、standard、token_id 和 min_balance，来源是服务器数据库。用户不能通过请求一个自己拥有的合约来替换受保护内容的门槛。规则写入还要检查内容所有者，不能只依赖前端隐藏按钮。

数量判断必须保留语义

ERC-721 某个 token 的 ownerOf 与合约级 balanceOf 含义不同；ERC-1155 对指定 id 返回数量。比如持有 1 份、规则要求 2 份时必须拒绝。缓存如果只存“曾通过”，min=1 的请求便可能污染 min=2 的请求。当前门禁保存持仓证据，并针对每次规则重新比较；并发和冷热缓存的结果应一致。

把证据钉在同一条链的同一个区块

门禁选择 safe/finalized 锚点，数量与探测读取要使用一致上下文，并验证锚点未变化。缓存年龄从原始 observedAt 计算；singleflight 等待者不能把自己醒来的时间当成新的观察时间。慢 RPC 也会消耗新鲜度预算。换一个节点，并不会让旧证据自动变新。

从返回值读拒绝语义

401 通常指凭据缺失或失效，403 指已识别身份但不满足授权；依赖无法验证属于5xx，不应伪装成“没有NFT”。默认closed模式下无规则拒绝；显式open是另一种运营策略，不能与关闭门禁混淆。门禁停用返回503，并不自动放行。

语义提醒： safe/finalized 是信任节点返回的链视图，既不代表本服务运行了轻客户端证明，也不保证链永不重组。

动手前先预测结果

给定同一个钱包持有 ERC-1155 的 id=7 共 1 份，依次请求 min=1、min=2，再逆序请求。预测结果并找到对应测试；最后指出跨链缓存键缺 chain_id 会造成什么错误。

展开：源码定位与练习命令

命令从仓库根执行。未注释的容器命令仅做观察；真实业务练习需要已准备的专用环境。

rg -n 'EvaluatePolicy|resolveQuantityObserved|verifyAnchorUnchanged' pkg/middleware/nft_gate.go
rg -n '^func TestNFTGate_|^func TestResolveQuantity_' pkg/middleware/nft_gate_quantity_test.go pkg/middleware/nft_gate_pinning_test.go

先合上参考答案，再自评
参考复述与检查重点

资格命题至少包含主体、链、合约、token 范围、数量阈值和时间上下文。每个请求必须在自己的规则下重新评估，不能沿用别的规则的放行结果。

05
HLS / capability / epoch

播放能力与撤销：把 70 秒讲准确

将昂贵的链读取与高频媒体请求分开，同时明确缓存和过期的代价。

0 / 3

Playlist 是严格的续权入口

阅读 routes.go：播放凭据验证在 JWT 前；流媒体组显式追加 JWT、按钱包的限流与 NFT 门禁。playlist 会重新解析规则及持仓证据，segment/init 等媒体读取凭有效能力与控制状态放行，不应每个分片都查询链。HLS 请求可以通过 URL 携带能力，但“存在 playback_token 参数”本身绝不放行。

70 秒是由三个预算相加得到的上界

在项目的约束与健康可观察链视图条件下：能力 TTL 60 秒 + 正证据年龄最多 5 秒 + 时钟容差最多 5 秒 = 70 秒。起点是 safe 视图已确认转移；从用户点击转移到链达到 safe 的等待时间不包含在内。自然过期测试不能替代转移撤销测试，浏览器暂停也不能证明服务器已经拒绝。

规则撤销、会话撤销与链转移是不同机制

规则版本、session/auth epoch、restore epoch 等控制信息参与令牌校验，处理规则更新、退出和恢复后的旧凭据。两种凭据同时出现时还要防止钱包与会话串用。应分别看 HTTP 和 gRPC 的独立判据，不让一个入口的通过替另一个入口背书。

不可撤回的东西： 能力是 bearer，URL 泄露会带来风险；钱包字段不要求每个分片都重新证明私钥控制。已经下载的媒体和缓冲无法远程收回，因此这不是 DRM。

动手前先预测结果

先操作下方撤销预算计算器，再解释把能力 TTL 改成 90 秒会破坏哪条约束。用一条时间轴区分提交转移、safe 确认、首次拒绝和播放器缓冲耗尽。

交互实验：撤销预算如何相加

仅为教学计算，不连接链、不修改服务配置。计时起点固定为 safe 视图确认转移。

60s
5s
5s
70s满足项目的各分量上限及 70 秒总预算。

提交交易 → 链等待：未知，另计 → safe 确认 → 本计算窗口 → 服务器拒绝新请求。已下载缓冲不在该窗口内。

06
封存 / FFmpeg / worker

上传、转码到真实 HLS：字节如何成为可播放内容

沿着原始字节走一遍，而不是把转码任务的 completed 当作真实播放。

0 / 3

原件先可信封存

从 upload 的 finalize/seal 路径读起：不能仅信 Content-Type、客户端 hash 或对象存储上传成功。服务端对原件计算摘要、检查实际大小并封存；后续覆盖不能静默改变同一原件的含义。读取限制要能区分“恰好达到上限”与“超限后被截断”。

解码器处理不可信输入

ffprobe 先验证允许的媒体条件，FFmpeg 负责实际编码和封装。外部命令要处理参数边界、超时、取消及整个进程组退出。实际执行者由 worker 的数据库 lease_owner 与日志归因，非 root、权限裁剪和不携带业务 JWT/链私钥减少解析不可信媒体的影响面。

播放必须验证完整字节路径

HLS manifest 的 EXTINF 来自真实产物，而非模板猜测；重写 URI 后，每个 playlist、segment、init 和 key 路径都应遵守授权及缓存策略。检查 Range、媒体类型、解码和浏览器 currentTime 推进，而不只检查 HTTP 200。服务使用发布后的 generation，不能把临时半成品交给播放器。

性能判断： FFmpeg、浏览器、压测器和 Go 应用应分开观察。当前 Mac 的 amd64 仿真会影响成本；不能从总 CPU 使用率直接推导“Go 慢”，也不能假定 Linux 容器已经接入 Mac VideoToolbox。

动手前先预测结果

画出“上传成功但转码失败”和“转码完成但浏览器解码失败”的两条路径。分别指出应保留的 DB 状态、worker 日志、manifest 和浏览器证据。

参考复述与检查重点

媒体验收需要从原件到浏览器的连续证据链。单个 completed 字段、单次 200 或一个可见播放器都不能证明媒体业务正确。

07
PostgreSQL / NATS / generation

跨资源一致性：任务、outbox 与旧 worker

消息可能重复、进程可能死亡，关键是副作用由谁拥有、何时对外可见。

0 / 3

数据库提交与消息发送之间没有魔法原子性

创建任务及 outbox 意图可在数据库事务内一起落盘；publisher 将意图投递到队列，失败后恢复。不能只“先写 DB，再发消息”，因为进程可能恰好在两步之间退出。NATS 提供交付，PostgreSQL 决定业务事实；原子数据库事务不能顺便覆盖对象存储和链上广播。

重复交付与旧执行者是两种风险

同一消息再次到来时，幂等状态检查防止重复推进。worker 领取任务后使用数据库 lease、心跳与 generation 约束写入；旧 worker 即便在网络恢复后继续运行，也不能覆盖新 generation 的最终状态。进程内 mutex 无法解决两台 worker 的竞争。

半成品必须隔离，发布必须有清晰切换点

转码输出先写到对应 generation 的私有对象前缀，验证后提交可见的发布状态。失败产物不能靠“最后一个写完的人获胜”。重试、耗尽、DLQ 和人工恢复也要保留状态含义，避免外部看起来成功而内部任务已经丢失。

比“exactly once”更准确的说法： 允许重复交付，通过持久化意图、幂等键、租约和发布协议限制可见副作用。是否达到具体语义，应由真实故障窗口验证。

动手前先预测结果

列出四个崩溃点：DB 提交前、提交后投递前、写对象后发布前、旧 worker 租约失效后。每个点写出恢复者、判据和允许重复的动作。

展开：源码定位与练习命令

参考复述与检查重点

把任务系统理解为“所有权转移 + 可恢复状态推进”。不同资源的副作用不可能凭一个事务全部回滚，因此必须显式定义重试、去重、隔离和发布边界。

08
safe/finalized / fallback / bulkhead

RPC、重组与缓存：正确性优先的降级

切换节点应该恢复可观察性，而不是降低授权要求。

0 / 3

多节点不等于多个独立真相

先检查配置的端点与目标链身份，再区分连接失败、超时、错误链、无法解析锚点和持仓确实不足。fallback 不能把“没有证据”转换成“没有限制”。safe/finalized 的返回仍来自受信任 RPC；公共节点可能延迟、限流或暂时不一致。

一条重组时间线

读取锚点 number/hash → 同锚点读取持仓 → 复核锚点 → 作出决定。如果期间 block hash 改变，应拒绝该组证据并重新观察。重组通知还要使相应链的证据缓存失效，不应该顺手清空另一条链的所有有效缓存。bounded evidence age 是时间约束，hash 校验是链历史身份约束，两者不能互相替代。

超时、重试和隔离需要总预算

一次请求的多个重试可能累积成很长延迟。研究 client 的 context、退避、fallback 和并发 bulkhead：限制并发可以防止故障 RPC 拖垮整个服务。同步等待者、共享连接与缓存键的资源预算同样需要明确。

正确降级： 主节点失败、备用节点提供合格证据时继续服务；所有节点都不能提供合格证据时拒绝授权，并把依赖错误与真实的无持仓区分开。

动手前先预测结果

对比三种结果：余额 0、RPC 超时、链身份不符。写出为何它们不应共用一条“用户没有 NFT”的 UI 提示；再描述备用节点比主节点落后时的安全处理。

09
Foundry / registry / nonce

合约与链写入：从 ABI 到广播不确定性

读懂已经接线的合约与交易恢复，不把一份 ABI 当成完整链上业务。

0 / 3

先区分三个合约的角色

DemoNFT 与 DemoERC1155 为演示/测试提供可控制的资产持仓；其中 permissionless mint 是 fixture 的选择，不是生产发行方案。ContentRegistry 记录平台侧内容登记与状态，它的摘要承诺不证明作品原创性或版权。理解 owner、creator 与状态迁移，再对照 Go ABI 和合约测试。

注册业务需要链下与链上共同推进

读 registry publisher 的 intent、nonce 分配、交易准备、原始签名事实持久化、广播与 receipt 恢复。广播超时可能只是响应丢失，此时不能把下一次操作当作全新业务重新分配 nonce。恢复应查询原交易，必要时重播原始字节，再核对链上结果。

mined 不自动等于稳定完成

receipt、canonical 区块与最终性约束需要结合状态机理解；发生重组时，先前观察的 receipt 可能失效。链上副作用不会因为一个数据库 lease 过期而被撤销，必须依靠业务幂等、持久化身份及恢复协议处理。

两项目分工： 在 StreamGate 中用内容登记解释这条链写入路径；在 ChainPulse 中更深入讲交易意图、结算和对账。这里不把两个仓库连接成已验收的联合系统。

动手前先预测结果

假设节点已经接收交易，但 HTTP 响应丢失。画出原始交易字节、nonce、intent 和 receipt 的关系，并指出直接重新签一笔新交易可能造成的重复副作用。

参考复述与检查重点

链写入的难点是不可撤销的外部副作用与不可靠响应。签名前后事实持久化、稳定业务身份及 canonical 观察，比“调用合约成功返回 hash”更值得解释。

10
Docker Desktop / profile / 可观测性

本机运行、诊断与资源预算

先证明系统如何运行，再讨论优化；把应用成本与验证成本分开。

0 / 3

区分三种运行入口

开发 helper、真实业务演示、完整 Docker 验收不是同一个命令。README 的 fullchain-deploy 需要预备 Linux 二进制；正式 verify-docker 由自己的工具链与隔离栈执行。首次下载、子模块准备、构建和已缓存启动必须分列。历史 57.14 秒是准备好镜像/编译器后的空演示卷启动，不能宣传为任意电脑冷启动。

本机预算是实验条件

Mac/Docker Desktop 的 CPU、RAM、架构仿真、后台容器集合和磁盘会影响结果。正式验收使用 profile 与同 daemon 独占约束，不能同时开多轮压测。仅增加核数或启用软件中的某个硬编选项，不足以证明收益；应分开观察 Go、FFmpeg、浏览器、压测器、构建与 Docker VM。

从症状走向证据

依次观察容器资源、Go CPU/heap、goroutine、数据库连接池、队列年龄和 RPC 延迟。pprof 在项目中有 debug/访问控制边界，不应为演示而向公共网络打开。采集器自身会扰动测量，所以不同 profile 应有意识地分开运行。

宿主机也是故障源： Mac 合盖休眠曾使 runner 丢失任务续租；caffeinate -i 防空闲休眠并不保证阻止合盖休眠。别把宿主休眠、网络中断和业务断言失败混成一个“flake”。

动手前先预测结果

写一份不超过六步的“CPU 高”诊断顺序；每步写明能证实什么、不能证实什么。再解释 /health、/ready 与真实播放分别覆盖哪一层。

展开：源码定位与练习命令

命令从仓库根执行。未注释的容器命令仅做观察；真实业务练习需要已准备的专用环境。

# 只读观察，不启动或清理服务
docker info --format '{{.Architecture}} {{.NCPU}} {{.MemTotal}}'
docker stats --no-stream

先合上参考答案，再自评
参考复述与检查重点

运行预算和测量环境是证据的一部分。容器存活、服务 ready、业务可用和满足 SLO 分别需要不同观察，不能用一个健康接口替代整条媒体路径。

11
M0–M8 / R01–R12 / SHA

测试、故障与同产物交付：如何判断真的完成

学习怎样识别一次真实的通过，而不仅是怎样把测试跑成绿色。

0 / 3

先找当前候选身份

区分 source SHA、应用 imageID、原始构建归档 hash、registry digest、CI run/attempt 与原始退出。文档提交记录了候选结果，不因此自动获得自己的同 SHA CI。合并保留代码内容也不意味着新合并 SHA 已完成全新验收。

测试层次回答不同问题

包测试验证局部行为；真实 PostgreSQL/Anvil 验证存储与链语义；双模式媒体验证完整业务；故障矩阵验证崩溃窗口；perf 验证完整场景矩阵及 reference 公平性；release 验证同产物、schema 兼容和失败恢复。Mock 通过、静态 UI 或 sleep 模拟负载都不能取代相应真实证据。

以原始退出和必需项为准

一个 loader exit 0 不代表48组矩阵已完成；52个CI job成功不代表剩余必需门通过。负例必须让坏实现失败，恢复又必须独立通过。取消的原始143不能被写成成功，摘要上传完成也不能代替完整 required-job 结果。恢复的RPO=0仅对应停写排空快照演练，不能推广为任意故障零丢失。

历史事实快照： d0f05152 的完整九阶段退出0、M0～M8齐全，run 41343759379 attempt3为55/55 success。这里只引用已保存证据，不重新执行CI，也不把这些数字迁移到任意新提交。

动手前先预测结果

拿一份摘要，独立找出其 candidateSHA、原始command.exit和缺失项策略。给它注入一个SKIP或错误身份，预测哪个原验证器应拒绝；不要修改真实归档。

展开：源码定位与练习命令

命令从仓库根执行。未注释的容器命令仅做观察；真实业务练习需要已准备的专用环境。

# 先读协议，不自动开启长跑
rg -n 'M0|M8|rawExitCode|candidateSHA' scripts/cd/generate_candidate_gates.py
rg -n 'R01|R12|required|rawExitCode' scripts/cd/summarize_ci_evidence.py
# 专用 clean checkout、Docker资源合格且无其他验收时，才选择单门：

参考复述与检查重点

验收是一组绑定身份且可重新核对的事实，不是一个成功字符串。长跑应在必要时执行一次；局部反馈选择最小相关门，历史原始失败始终保留。

12
项目讲解 / 设计取舍 / 现场修改

从“看懂项目”到“能够独立负责”

把代码、故障、取舍和自己的贡献，组织成能经得住追问的叙事。

0 / 3

三段式项目介绍

30秒：Go实现基于链上持仓的媒体访问控制。3分钟：讲身份、规则、同块证据、能力与媒体路径。10分钟：选一个真实缺陷，讲失败现象、最小复现、取舍、修复及证据。先解释问题，再提技术名词和测量值。

准备三个反事实问题

如果每个segment都查链，会发生什么？如果能力不绑定规则版本，会发生什么？如果DB提交后进程在发消息前死亡，会发生什么？能从代码推导答案，比背“用了Redis、NATS、gRPC”更能证明你理解系统。

独立完成一个小改动

在隔离分支选择一项边界明确的工作，例如增加一个错误签名类型负例、追踪一次慢RPC的观察时间，或补充一个lease过期后的状态断言。先解释失败应如何出现，再写代码和相关测试。不要为了练习修改已冻结候选或开启整链长跑。

个人贡献必须真实： AI生成代码、本人阅读理解、本人做出设计决策、本人定位修复，属于不同事实。根据你实际完成的动作讲述，不把简历中的待确认占位或这份自评勾选当作已经证明的履历。

动手前先预测结果

合上文档录一段3分钟讲解；请另一人追问“为什么不是另一种方案”。再现场画出一条转移撤销时间线和一个outbox崩溃窗口。

13
ERC-1271 / 续验 / Sepolia

A 签名、C 登录：把合约钱包接进真实权限链

把签名控制者、登录主体、资产持有人和链状态分别追到权威来源。

0 / 3

先固定身份，再谈签名格式

浏览器连接控制者 A，SIWE address、JWT subject、NFT 查询和播放能力主体都是合约 C。后端在已配置链上读取账户代码，EOA 才做地址恢复；已部署合约将原始签名字节交给 isValidSignature。后端支持有上限的 opaque bytes，不假定所有合约都采用65字节ECDSA。自有 Study1271Wallet 只接受单EOA owner的64/65字节签名；它不能代表任意钱包的策略。

代码、验签与区块身份必须是一份观察

同一 safe 区块的 code、eth_call 与 number/hash 复核共同构成证明。500000 gas、8KiB签名、20KiB JSON及5秒总deadline是本项目上限；RPC回退、排队不能各自重置5秒。错误magic、revert、异常ABI及依赖错误均不能兜底成EOA成功。本轮拒绝6492包装签名、7702委托账户和合约EIP-712。

一次签名成功，为什么仍要续验？

合约策略会变。C 的 access 租期最多60秒，绝对会话期限最多2小时；刷新复验服务器保存的原登录证明，再用Redis Lua同时比较epoch、完整旧记录、JTI与版本。绝对期限、原proof与身份不能滑动。缺失proof、撤销、旧JTI和依赖不可读都不能重建有效会话。能力exp受签发它的那枚access token约束，后来刷新不能替旧能力延寿；segment仍走便宜的能力/共享控制状态检查。

公开路径用六笔交易，两个模式共享资产变化

固定 Ethereum Sepolia 11155111：部署N和C → mint给A → A转给B → mint给C → A经C.execute转给B。两种模式在每个持有/转移阶段分别取证，避免各部署一套。第二条路径中A的相关持仓为0，证明C才是主体。本人操作钱包；得到交易hash后仍需canonical receipt、可信safe持仓及真实媒体观察。UNKNOWN先对账，不自动重发；主备RPC不能退化为单端点。

完成边界：本单元解释候选c38dc4bc的实现（四份源码与当前候选d267ad44逐字节相同）。完整同C默认CI和原始all已通过；公开Sepolia已留证真实MetaMask SIWE登录、一次拒签、六笔真实交易与eoa-held链上oracle；双模式公开媒体、完整TN矩阵与其余阶段证据仍待完成；不能勾选学习自测来代替业务验收。safe→拒绝的70秒预算不包含链确认等待，签名策略撤销与NFT转移需各自证据。

先预测，再看源码

画出 A/C/N 的控制权与资产图。模拟“续验RPC成功后、Redis CAS之前发生logout”，说明哪个比较拒绝旧结果；再说明RPC超时为何应返回503而非403。

展开：只读源码定位
rg -n 'VerifyWalletProof|ObservedAt|ErrWalletUnsupported' pkg/web3/signature/account.go
rg -n 'contractRecord|requireWalletIssuance|CompareAndSwap' pkg/service/auth_contract_wallet.go
rg -n 'rotateWalletSession|CurrentJTI|AuthEpoch' pkg/storage/wallet_session.go
rg -n 'isValidSignature|onlyOwner|execute' contracts/src/Study1271Wallet.sol

在冻结候选工作树阅读。本单元不提供自动签名或广播命令；真实运行按TRANSITION_PLAN.md §10使用原CI产物及公开预检。

固定候选源码 · 可离线阅读

学完后，我能独立做到

先合上参考答案，再自评
参考复述与检查重点

身份由C的链上策略裁决；签名状态与NFT资格分别变化。授权期限必须落到原始凭据、不可滑动的会话和权威CAS。公开路径的UI、交易hash、receipt、safe持仓、原媒体字节与浏览器解码分别回答不同问题，任何单项都不能替代完整闭环。

可以说明必须同时说明不要推导为
钱包登录与 NFT 门禁服务器规则、同链同块证据、会话/能力约束无须信任、所有钱包类型或所有链已验收
safe 确认后的 ≤70s 访问撤销预算能力60s + 正证据5s + 容差5s；链等待另计从提交交易起70s内链确认，或播放器立即停止

AI辅助完成的工程项目本人理解、决策、排障和现场修改分别核实自动等同于多年生产经历或独立设计全部实现

遇到这些词，先翻译成一个具体问题

词在本项目中的实际问题
身份 / 授权你控制哪个账户？这个账户此刻有资格读取这个内容吗？
nonce / challenge这段签名是否为本次登录生成，是否已被消费？
anchor / observedAt哪个number/hash的链状态？证据从什么时候开始变旧？
capability / bearer谁持有短期访问凭据？凭据约束了哪些资源与版本？
rule version / epoch规则、会话或恢复边界变化后，旧凭据是否必须失效？
lease / generation谁现在有任务写入权？旧执行者还能覆盖结果吗？
outbox / DLQ哪些意图尚待交付？重试耗尽后由谁观察并恢复？
intent / signed attempt业务想做什么？已签交易的nonce/hash/raw是什么？
canonical / finality观察的区块是否仍在当前链上？达到哪种稳定性政策？
digest / imageID / SHA源码、构建归档、运行镜像与发布产物分别是谁？
RTO / RPO该次演练恢复多快？按什么写入与快照条件谈数据损失？
fail closed证据或依赖无法确认时，拒绝访问还是意外放行？

让 AI 做教练，保留你的判断

01 先画图

写出身份、权威状态、外部副作用和失败窗口。

02 再追源码

每个重要箭头找实际函数，优先读装配与拒绝分支。

03 先预测再验证

说清坏改动应让哪个测试失败，再做最小相关实验。

04 合上材料复述

解释为什么这样设计、放弃了什么、边界在哪里。

我正在学习 StreamGate 的【单元/函数】。
请先问我三个由浅到深的问题，等我回答后再评价。
让我画出调用链、状态权威和一个失败窗口。
用当前源码/测试核对我的回答，指出我混淆的概念。
不要替我完成练习；不要把 mock、历史成功或可见 UI 当成当前验收。
最后给我一个范围很小、能独立修改和验证的练习。

三项综合练习

  1. 授权练习：从持仓数量不足、规则变化、logout各选一例，解释两种入口的独立拒绝判据。
  2. 持久化练习：为一个真实崩溃窗口画恢复图，区分可重复动作、唯一事实与不可撤销副作用。
  3. 工程练习：选择一次真实CPU热点或错误处理问题，保存前后条件、证据和未改善部分，不预先承诺性能收益。

新练习使用隔离分支和专用资源；当前已有有效验收时，不为学习同时启动另一轮完整Docker/CI。运行前读仓库约束，不删除其他项目容器。

离线文档与源码阅读区

14份项目资料 · 25段源码

这些是指定仓库快照的原文或精确节选。源文件SHA256用于回查，不证明文档中的每个历史描述都仍有效；教程正文已按代码解释关键差异。旧架构文档可能把早期缓存或认证顺序写得过于简单。优先顺序：当前代码 → 当前项目规则 → 对应SHA的验收证据 → 历史说明。

00 — Charter

Type: identity · Last verified: 2026-10-21 @ df253ef

What StreamGate is

A Go platform that gates video playback on on-chain NFT ownership:

wallet sign-in → ownership proven at a safe block → short-lived playback capability → HLS

Module github.com/rtcdance/streamgate, version 1.0.6. One codebase, two topologies: a monolith for development and demos, nine microservices for a prod-like deployment.

Why it exists

This is primarily a portfolio and interview vehicle for Web3 backend roles, not a production service with an operating user base. That single fact drives most trade-offs:

  • The core flow must be demonstrable end-to-end — real wallet, real chain, real HLS — not merely unit-tested.
  • Every security claim must survive questioning and be backed by a reproducible check.
  • Depth beats breadth. A narrow, correct, well-evidenced path is worth more than many half-wired features.

Audience

  1. Interviewers and reviewers reading the repository and asking "why".
  2. The owner, returning to the system after time away.
  3. AI agents changing code under the rules in CLAUDE.md.

Success criteria

  • A reviewer can go from clone to a working gated stream using DEPLOY.md.
  • The authorization path fails closed and is pinned by regression tests.
  • Acceptance evidence is reproducible from Docker or CI, never from a host build.
  • The owner can explain every major decision without opening the code.

Non-goals

Non-goal Why
ERC-4337 / account abstraction pkg/web3/account_abstraction.go is unused; it is not a proof point
IPFS / decentralized storage the upload path is MinIO/S3; decentralization is not claimed
P2P networking or token economics the platform is centralized by design
Production capacity claims only fixed-budget local measurements exist
Speculative features, premature abstraction forbidden by project rules

Constraints

  • Docker-first: a host build is a fast compile check; acceptance comes from the Docker stack or CI.
  • Solo owner plus AI agents: context must be written down, not remembered.
  • Single plan document: TRANSITION_PLAN.md. No process documents.
  • Dependency gate: a new module needs approval and a DEPENDENCY_APPROVAL.md entry.

10 — Architecture

Type: architecture · Last verified: 2026-10-21 @ df253ef Authority: docs/ARCHITECTURE.md and the code. This file is the recall summary.

Shape

Microkernel plus plugins. pkg/core owns the kernel, GenericPlugin, config, the event bus and logging. pkg/plugins/* are thin wrappers; business logic lives in pkg/service/.

cmd/monolith/streamgate        all 9 plugins in one process     :8080  (compose :18080)
cmd/microservices/<name>       9 standalone mains, own wiring    api-gateway :28080
pkg/plugins/<name>/plugin.go   lifecycle + route registration
pkg/service/<domain>/          business logic — the real layer
pkg/gateway/routes.go          THE route table; middleware order lives here

Request path that matters

routes.go
  └─ streaming group: PlaybackTokenAuth → JWT → NFTGate
       ├─ segment / init / key : a valid capability is sufficient (no chain query)
       └─ manifest.m3u8        : re-resolve rules + re-verify holding (renewal point)
            └─ web3/nft verifier → RPC at safe/finalized
                 └─ streamingsvc delivers HLS

The asymmetry is deliberate: playback is cheap, renewal is strict. See D-007.

Layering rules

  1. Business logic never lives in a plugin or an HTTP handler. Plugins wire; services decide.
  2. Every route registers in pkg/gateway/routes.go. A route added elsewhere is a bug.
  3. The authorization subject (chain, contract, token id, minimum balance) comes from content_gating_rules in PostgreSQL — never from the request.
  4. Microservice mains wire themselves. core.RunMicroservice() exists but has no callers.

Topology

Mode Containers API Frontend
Monolith 7 :18080 :18000
Microservices 9 services (api-gateway + 8 backends) + infra :28080 :18001
Full chain 17 (docker-compose.fullchain.yml, both side by side) both both

Backing services: PostgreSQL 15, Redis 7, MinIO/S3, NATS, Consul (microservices only). Async: MemoryEventBus in the monolith, NATSEventBus in microservices (built with -tags nats_integration).

Extension recipe

  • New domain: pkg/service/<domain>/ + pkg/plugins/<name>/plugin.go + a route in routes.go + a migration. Keep the plugin to wiring only.
  • New chain: add it under web3.chains in config/config.yaml with an rpc_urls fallback list. A single endpoint is not acceptable.
  • New gate rule type: extend pkg/service/gating/ and pkg/middleware/nft_gate.go together. The gate must remain fail-closed.

Deliberate absences

pkg/web3/account_abstraction.go and pkg/web3/contract/registry.go are complete but never constructed. They are not dead code waiting to be wired up — they are out of scope (see D-011).

20 — Security Invariants

Type: invariants · Last verified: 2026-10-21 @ df253ef These are not preferences. Breaking one is a release blocker.

The invariants

# Invariant Enforced in Why
S1 Never deliver a manifest or segment without an authorization decision — fresh ownership at the playlist, a valid capability for media pkg/middleware/nft_gate.go, pkg/gateway/routes.go it is the product's only reason to exist
S2 The authorization subject comes from server-side rules, never the request resolveGatingRules otherwise the caller chooses what to prove
S3 The gate fails closed NFTGateMiddleware an outage must not become a bypass
S4 Ownership is read at safe or finalized, never latest pkg/web3/nft/verifier.go a reorg must not grant access
S5 Evidence is same-chain and age-bounded (default 5s) resolveAnchor, evidenceAgeBound a stale read ignores transfers and rule changes
S6 Challenges are consumed atomically Redis Lua in pkg/storage/redis_challenge.go, called by the auth service check-then-use would allow replay
S7 A playback token is a signed, wallet-bound capability — presence alone never authorizes GeneratePlaybackToken, ValidatePlaybackToken HLS.js cannot set Authorization on media requests
S8 token_type and audience are checked, not just the signature ParsePlaybackToken an access JWT must not work as a playback token
S9 An empty wallet never receives a playback token GeneratePlaybackToken it would be an unauthenticated success
S10 Every chain read has an RPC fallback chain pkg/web3/, config rpc_urls a single endpoint is a single point of failure

Gate decision matrix

Condition Result
manifest.m3u8, rules exist, holding sufficient allow; mint or renew capability
manifest.m3u8, holding insufficient 403
segment / init / key, valid capability allow, no chain query
segment / init / key, missing or invalid capability 401
no rules, mode closed (default) 403 GATING_REQUIRED
no rules, mode open (explicit opt-in) allow
rule resolution error 5xx
gate disabled 503 GATING_DISABLED
no authenticated wallet 401

Threat model

  • Replay — challenge reuse, token reuse across content, reuse across credential kinds. Defenses: atomic nonce consumption, content_id binding, token_type plus audience, jti revocation.
  • Reorg / stale read — granting on a block that later disappears. Defenses: safe/finalized, anchor re-check, 5s evidence bound, reorg invalidation.
  • Confused deputy — the client supplies contract or token id. Defense: rules come from the database.
  • Transfer or rule change mid-playback — a capability would outlive the right. Defense: the playlist is the renewal point and always re-queries, and the capability itself is capped at 60s.
  • "Disabled" as a bypass — treating the off switch as allow. Defense: 503.

Review checklist

  • [ ] Did I add a route outside routes.go, or outside the streaming middleware chain?
  • [ ] Does any new path return media bytes before nft_verified is set?
  • [ ] Did I introduce latest, an untagged eth_call, or a single RPC URL?
  • [ ] Does any failure branch return 2xx?
  • [ ] Is an authorization input taken from a param, query or body instead of the rules table?
  • [ ] Is there a regression test that fails if the check is removed?

30 — Decision Log

Type: decisions · Last verified: 2026-10-21 @ df253ef Append-only. A changed mind is a new entry that supersedes the old one.

D-001 — Monolith first, microservices only when scale demands

Status: accepted · Evidence: cmd/monolith, cmd/microservices

Context: Nine services is a lot of wiring for a system whose value is the authorization path.

Decision: Ship one binary that loads every plugin; keep standalone mains as a second topology over the same code.

Why: A demo must start in one command, and the split is only justified by independent scaling — which this project cannot claim.

Consequences: Two wiring paths to keep in sync. Plugins must stay free of cross-plugin assumptions. The monolith is the default acceptance target.

D-002 — Plugins are thin wrappers; pkg/service/ holds the logic

Status: accepted · Evidence: pkg/plugins/*/plugin.go, pkg/service/

Context: The microkernel makes it easy to put logic in a plugin, which would make it unreachable from another topology.

Decision: A plugin registers lifecycle and routes; every decision lives in a service.

Why: One logic layer, two topologies, no duplication.

Consequences: New features start in pkg/service/. A plugin over ~20 lines is a smell.

D-003 — The NFT gate fails closed

Status: accepted · Evidence: pkg/middleware/nft_gate.go

Context: A gate that opens on error is worse than no gate, because it looks safe.

Decision: No rules → 403; resolver error → 5xx; gate disabled → 503. GatingMode defaults to closed; open is an explicit operator opt-in.

Why: Availability must never be traded for authorization.

Consequences: Misconfiguration breaks playback loudly. That is intended.

D-004 — Read ownership at safe / finalized, never latest

Status: accepted · Evidence: pkg/web3/nft/verifier.go

Context: A balance at latest can be undone by a reorg after access is granted.

Decision: Default to a finalized-class block tag; re-check the anchor before renewing a playlist.

Why: Access must be granted on state that will not disappear.

Consequences: Slightly older state, slightly higher latency. Accepted.

D-005 — Consume challenges with Redis Lua

Status: accepted · Evidence: pkg/service/auth_wallet.go

Context: Read-then-delete is a TOCTOU window; two concurrent requests could both consume one challenge.

Decision: Consume atomically inside a Lua script.

Why: Replay protection must be atomic to be real.

Consequences: Redis is on the critical path for sign-in. It already was.

D-006 — Playback authorization is a capability carried in the query string

Status: accepted · Evidence: pkg/middleware/playback_token_auth.go

Context: HLS.js cannot attach an Authorization header to segment requests.

Decision: Mint a short-lived, wallet-bound token with an explicit token_type and audience; validate it cryptographically in middleware.

Why: The transport constraint is fixed by the browser, not by us.

Consequences: Tokens appear in URLs and logs — hence short TTL, wallet binding, content binding, revocation and audience checks. Presence alone never authorizes.

D-007 — The playlist is the renewal point; segments are cheap

Status: accepted · Evidence: pkg/middleware/nft_gate.go

Context: Querying the chain on every segment would make playback expensive and fragile; never re-querying would let a transferred NFT keep playing.

Decision: A valid capability serves segments without a chain read. Every manifest.m3u8 request re-resolves rules and re-verifies the current holding against fresh evidence.

Why: It puts the expensive check at the natural renewal boundary.

Consequences: Worst-case staleness is the capability TTL — 60s default and hard ceiling — not one segment. The playlist refresh is the earliest point a revoked right is noticed. Documented and accepted.

D-008 — Authorization subject comes from the database, not the request

Status: accepted · Evidence: resolveGatingRules

Context: Accepting contract or token_id from the caller lets them prove ownership of something cheaper than what they are watching.

Decision: Rules live in content_gating_rules; the request supplies only the content id and the credential.

Why: It removes an entire class of confused-deputy bugs.

Consequences: Every gated content needs a rule row. The demo seed exists for this.

D-009 — Docker and CI are the acceptance authority

Status: accepted · Evidence: Makefile, .github/workflows/

Context: Host builds hide tag differences, missing services and image wiring.

Decision: make verify-docker and make fullchain-test produce evidence; CI is the final gate. A host build is a pre-check only.

Why: "Works on my machine" is not evidence for a reviewer.

Consequences: Local acceptance needs the two prebuilt Linux binaries and a 17-container stack. Slower, but defensible.

D-010 — Contract bytecode must be reproducible

Status: accepted · Evidence: contracts/foundry.toml, scripts/registry-manifest.sh

Context: solc embeds absolute remappings in the metadata hash, so a host build and a /contracts build produced different bytecode and the services refused to start.

Decision: bytecode_hash = "none", and regenerate the manifest record after any contract change.

Why: Deployment must not depend on the builder's filesystem path.

Consequences: Contract changes require a manifest refresh — a known startup failure if forgotten.

D-011 — Account abstraction stays out of scope

Status: accepted · Evidence: pkg/web3/account_abstraction.go, plan appendix D

Context: ERC-4337 types and entry-point validation exist but nothing constructs them.

Decision: Do not wire them up, and do not delete them to look tidy.

Why: It is not a proof point for this project and would dilute the security story.

Consequences: Reviewers may ask. The answer is "deliberately out of scope", not "unfinished".

D-012 — One plan document, no process documents

Status: accepted · Evidence: CLAUDE.md → Documentation

Context: Status reports and handovers rot, multiply, and contradict the plan.

Decision: TRANSITION_PLAN.md is the only plan and evidence log. Reference material lives under docs/. This memory/ tree holds recall, not status.

Why: A single timeline is auditable; many are not.

Consequences: Finished work is recorded as evidence in the plan, not as a new file.

Adding a decision

Copy the shape above. Keep Context / Decision / Why / Consequences — the why is the part the code cannot tell you. Give it the next D-xxx id and link the files that enforce it.

70 — Glossary and Map

Type: reference · Last verified: 2026-10-21 @ df253ef

Project IDs

ID Meaning
SG-00 … SG-17 Work items in TRANSITION_PLAN.md §5. P0 = blocking, P1 = required, P2 = conditional
§8.x Evidence sections of the plan. More trustworthy than the checkboxes
R01 … R12 Requirement traceability ids used by SG-15

Domain terms

Term Meaning here
Gating rule A row in content_gating_rules: chain, contract, token id, standard, minimum balance, active flag
Rule version Monotonic version of a content's rule set; bound into capabilities and read through the rule-version authority so a rule edit can invalidate authorization
Anchor The block (number + hash) at which ownership was proven; re-checked before renewal
Evidence age Maximum age of an ownership read before it must be re-fetched; default 5s
Playback token / capability Short-lived JWT (token_type=playback, 60s default and ceiling) bound to wallet, content, rule version and optionally a session
Access token Ordinary login JWT with token_type=access; must never pass playback validation
Fail closed Errors and missing configuration deny access rather than allow it
Safe / finalized Ethereum block tags used for ownership reads; latest is forbidden
Reorg Chain reorganization that can invalidate a previously observed holding
sigchain pkg/service/sigchain — routes signature verification to EVM (secp256k1/EIP-191) or Solana (ed25519)
Anvil Local Foundry chain, id 31337, hosting DemoNFT, DemoERC1155 and ContentRegistry
HLS HTTP Live Streaming; manifests (.m3u8) plus segments, served by streamingsvc

Core file map

cmd/monolith/streamgate/main.go         entry, plugin loading
pkg/plugins/auth/plugin.go              auth plugin, route registration
pkg/service/auth_wallet.go              wallet sign-in, playback tokens
pkg/web3/signature/                     EIP-191 / EIP-712 / SIWE verification
pkg/middleware/nft_gate.go              gate, rule resolution, fail-closed policy
pkg/middleware/playback_token_auth.go   capability validation ahead of JWT
pkg/web3/nft/verifier.go                ownership, ERC-165 detection
pkg/gateway/routes.go                   the route table and middleware order
pkg/service/streamingsvc/streaming.go   manifest delivery
pkg/service/transcoding/transcoding.go  FFmpeg pipeline and worker pool

40 — Operations

Type: operations · Last verified: 2026-10-21 @ df253ef Full command list: CLAUDE.md. This is the recall layer: what matters and what bites.

Environment

docker-compose.fullchain.yml — 17 containers, all healthchecked. The monolith and api-gateway quick images COPY prebuilt Linux binaries; the deploy script does not build them.

GOOS=linux GOARCH=$(go env GOARCH) CGO_ENABLED=0 go build -o deploy/docker/streamgate-linux ./cmd/monolith/streamgate
GOOS=linux GOARCH=$(go env GOARCH) CGO_ENABLED=0 go build -o deploy/docker/api-gateway-linux ./cmd/microservices/api-gateway
Host port Service
18080 / 19090 monolith HTTP / gRPC
28080 api-gateway
18000 / 18001 h5-demo (monolith view / gateway view)
18545 Anvil — chain 31337, DemoNFT + ContentRegistry
15432 / 16379 / 19000-19001 / 14222 / 28500 PostgreSQL / Redis / MinIO / NATS / Consul

Verification ladder

Rung Command What it proves
0 go build ./cmd/monolith/streamgate it compiles; nothing more
1 make test unit and integration behavior on the host
2 VERIFY_STAGE=unit make verify-docker the unit gate inside the real image
3 make verify-docker all nine stages; all requires candidate mode and a clean tree
4 make fullchain-test the full acceptance script against the running stack
5 CI (ci.yml, build.yml) authoritative acceptance

Rungs 0–1 are pre-checks. Evidence is a CI run id or a commit hash, never a host run.

Traps

Symptom Cause Fix
fullchain-deploy says deploy/docker/streamgate-linux: not found quick Dockerfiles COPY prebuilt binaries build the two Linux binaries first
The gRPC checks fail with exit status 1 default GRPC_TARGET=monolith:9090 is compose-internal GRPC_TARGET=localhost:19090 make fullchain-test
Image build fails fetching github.com Dockerfile defaults GOPROXY=direct VERIFY_GOPROXY=https://goproxy.cn,direct make verify-docker
Build stalls or OOMs on an 8GB Docker VM six or more parallel go build -a build services serially, one docker compose build at a time
Startup: runtime code hash mismatch solc embedded absolute remappings keep bytecode_hash = "none"; regenerate the manifest
Microservice events error outside Docker host make build-all lacks -tags nats_integration run the service from its image
NFT verification returns a wrong answer latest block tag use safe / finalized

Evidence discipline

  • A green host test is a pre-check. Do not present it as acceptance.
  • Capture the commit hash alongside any run. History was rewritten twice with git filter-repo on 2026-10-21, so SHAs quoted in older docs may not resolve. Check with git cat-file -e <sha>^{commit}; prefer plan section numbers or tree hashes when citing evidence.
  • A full make verify-docker run (VERIFY_STAGE=all) requires a clean tree; commit first, then verify. A single stage may run on a dirty tree.
  • Delete generated artifacts when done: bin/, out/, cache/, target/, coverage.out, deploy/docker/*-linux, scripts/h5-demo-acceptance/reports/. Never delete .workbuddy/.

Acceptance environment (2026-10-24)

The owner has only a Mac, no Linux server. Local Docker Desktop is the default acceptance environment, including the full nine-stage DAG. The controller selects VERIFY_ENVIRONMENT_PROFILE=desktop-local on macOS: >=8 VM CPUs, >=15 GiB usable RAM (16 GiB configured), >=100 GiB free measured inside the Docker filesystem. The native-linux profile keeps its amd64 / 32 GiB / dedicated-daemon checks.

  • Keep linux/amd64 until the pinned ARM solc/toolchain is independently ported.
  • Run stages and builds sequentially; shared Go cache is opt-in, tests use -count=1.
  • Other acceptance runs are forbidden; background business containers are reported, never stopped without authorization. Performance manifests bind their IDs, the execution profile/platform and VM identity, and include a load snapshot.
  • Equal background IDs do not prove equal load. Mac performance is a local, potentially emulated/shared-host observation, never production capacity proof.
  • All/perf/release automatically freeze the source; same-SHA CI remains separate.
  • No remote prod-srs acceptance; that environment was purged on 2026-10-23.

For same-SHA CI, the acceptance job can use this Mac as a self-hosted runner. STREAMGATE_ACCEPTANCE_RUNNER selects its dedicated label; STREAMGATE_ACCEPTANCE_PROFILE=desktop-local selects the preflight profile. Installation/registration, repository settings, push and dispatch require explicit authorization. Local results do not stand in for that job or registry receipts.

Self-hosted runner uplink (2026-10-25)

The Mac's upload path measured ~111 KiB/s (5 MiB to a public speed-test endpoint; the runner's own /proc/net/dev agreed at ~113 KiB/s during a cache save). Downloads are an order of magnitude faster. This asymmetry, not quota, sets the cost of every CI design here:

  • Never let a job upload a multi-hundred-MB cache. actions/setup-go defaults to caching a 1.2 GiB Go cache, so cache: false is deliberate; cache-to on the buildx image builds is likewise removed (a mode=min export took 5,823s and then failed on the one-hour cache token).
  • Candidate images still have to travel to the Mac, so the transport stages are the slow part of a full DAG. Do not add uploads that nothing consumes.
  • Expect a failed large upload to surface as a bare EOF from the registry; the transport helper retries a bounded number of times before it gives up.

Same-SHA retry after a transient third-party flake (2026-10-25)

A failed job that never reached the candidate's own assertions is retried on the same SHA, not by dispatching a new commit: rerun-failed-jobs re-runs the failed jobs and their dependents, so the image/acceptance stages that were skipped still execute, and the run keeps one SHA and one evidence chain. The transport consumer resolves g<run>-a<attempt>-<name> from max-attempt downwards, so an attempt-2 consumer still accepts attempt-1 artifacts.

Two things that waste time here:

  • gh run rerun <id> --failed exits 0 even when the API refuses with 403 This workflow is already running. Confirm with gh api -X POST .../rerun-failed-jobs (non-zero on refusal) or by checking that run_attempt incremented.
  • scripts/cd/verify_environment.sh defaults to profile=native-linux, which demands a native amd64 Linux host with 32 GiB RAM. On this Mac it must be run as VERIFY_ENVIRONMENT_PROFILE=desktop-local; the CI job gets that from the STREAMGATE_ACCEPTANCE_PROFILE repository variable.

The runner container shares the DinD's network namespace (--network container:<dind>), which is why job steps reach stack ports published inside the DinD at localhost and why services: port mappings work. It also means only one runner container may run at a time: every runner mounts the same /Users/mingo/ci-work:/work, so two would share _work paths and overwrite each other's checkouts. Swapping sg-linux-runner for sg-linux-runner-04 is a stop-then-start, never both.

50 — Status Snapshot

Type: status · Snapshot: 2026-11-02 · Verified code candidate: d0f05152edc1fb404cb2d78b734c95244e1a7a4e Documentation records the candidate; its commit does not inherit candidate CI. Recheck live state before new work.

Current scope

SG-00 through SG-15 engineering and M6 AI materials are complete with local Mac/Docker Desktop boundaries. No new feature work is required to close this delivery.

Authoritative evidence

  • One complete same-candidate Docker all: original outer exit 0, nine stages, M0–M8 and R01–R12 index complete.
  • CI run 41343759379, attempt 3: 55/55 jobs success, no skip; acceptance, evidence and Publishable succeeded. Publication means promotion eligibility, not production deployment.
  • Independent regeneration of the CI summary is byte-identical to CI: SHA256 516e0f3abf37b182e8f04c05760519a2287b6fd836f16e7459e39b05ef7dce88.
  • Four finite counterexamples qualified by exact implementation/test/harness identity under plan §8.51. Original dadb019b run identity and raw exits remain historical.

  • Plan §8.86 owns commands, hashes, manifests, measurements and evidence locations. Do not restart green CI to update this snapshot.

Operational rules learned

  • Keep the Mac lid open. A task-scoped caffeinate assertion prevents idle sleep, not guaranteed lid-close sleep. Actual lid-close sleep interrupted a prior CI job lease.
  • Initialize every exact gitlink recursively before demo startup. A container being up does not prove contract or business readiness.
  • A cancelled CI shell and its local controller can diverge; preserve original failures, inspect live state, and never relabel residual work as a successful CI job.
  • One dispatcher owns each frozen candidate. Do not reset retry budgets merely by changing an error classifier. No paid quota increase or new parallel acceptance on this Mac.

Boundaries and separately scoped work

  • Mac fixed-budget/shared-host measurements do not establish native Linux or production capacity. Testnet and production deployment are unverified.
  • Personal contribution claims, presentation, recording and applications remain human confirmation/work. No fabricated sign-off.
  • SG-16/17 remain conditional and unstarted. ERC-4337 is out of scope; IPFS is not wired into upload.
  • Local reliability drafts 12e1d86e/f501c92f were not included in the accepted candidate; adopting them later requires a new candidate. They are not part of the completed candidate’s CI claims.

Next action

Keep the accepted candidate frozen. Reproduce the prepared demo or start a new explicitly scoped task; do not reopen the completed all/CI cycle based on historical pending notes.

StreamGate Development Guide

NFT-gated streaming platform in Go. Core flow: wallet sign-in → NFT ownership check → protected HLS streaming.

  • Microkernel plugin architecture, dual-mode: monolith (dev) or 9 microservices (prod)
  • 11 build targets (make build-all) · 340 tracked Go test files · ~71% coverage (verify-docker unit gate) · 60% CI floor

Stack

Go 1.26 · Gin · go-ethereum + solana-go · PostgreSQL 15 · Redis 7 · MinIO/S3 · NATS · Consul · HLS/DASH via FFmpeg · Zap + Prometheus + OpenTelemetry · gRPC · Docker Compose + Kubernetes · testify + uber/mock

Project knowledge (tool-neutral)

Every AI tool must start from these two root directories before a matching task.

  • memory/ — long-term recall: charter, architecture, security invariants, decisions, operations, status, glossary. Read memory/README.md, then memory/60-collaboration.md and the file matching the task. Memory is recall, not documentation or a plan: on conflict the code wins, then this file, then TRANSITION_PLAN.md.
  • skills/ — reusable procedures. Read skills/INDEX.md and the matching skills/<name>/SKILL.md. This is the only skill catalog.

Both are canonical at the repository root. .claude/skills and .agents/skills are pointers only; do not add authoritative content there. Update memory/ when a durable decision, invariant, operational fact or status changes — protocol in memory/README.md. Acceptance: dev-machine-acceptance; remote execution: remote-dev-machine.

Commands

Build, run and acceptance all happen in Docker. The host targets below are for fast local edits only — acceptance evidence comes from the Docker stack or CI, never from a host build.

# Build / run
make build-monolith        # single binary
make build-all             # all 11 binaries
make run-monolith          # build + run on :8080 (also: dev, dev-setup)
make demo                  # infra (PG+Redis+MinIO) + monolith on :8080

# Test
make test                  # all tests, -race, coverage HTML
make test-ci               # CI mode (60% floor)
make test-anvil            # Foundry/Anvil Web3 integration tests
make test-testnet          # needs SEPOLIA_RPC

# Quality / codegen
make lint                  # golangci-lint (also: lint-fix, fmt)
make mocks                 # go generate ./...
make proto-gen             # protobuf stubs

# Docker / deploy
make fullchain-deploy      # full stack; needs deploy/docker/*-linux prebuilt (see below)
docker compose -f docker-compose.fullchain.yml up -d   # start without rebuilding (down to stop)
make docker-build          # images (also: docker-bake)
make demo-down             # stop demo infra and clean up
make migrate-up            # also: migrate-down, migrate-reset
make k8s-deploy            # also: k8s-status

# Docker acceptance (evidence, not host builds)
make verify-docker         # all stages (needs a clean tree); single stage: VERIFY_STAGE=unit make verify-docker
make fullchain-test        # 71-check acceptance against the running stack

# Contracts (Foundry)
make contracts-test        # also: contracts-build, contracts-deploy-anvil

Docker dev/test environment

docker-compose.fullchain.yml is the local dev/test environment: 17 containers, every one healthchecked. make fullchain-deploy starts it; it needs the two Linux binaries the "quick" Dockerfiles COPY (the deploy script does not build them):

GOOS=linux GOARCH=$(go env GOARCH) CGO_ENABLED=0 go build -o deploy/docker/streamgate-linux ./cmd/monolith/streamgate
GOOS=linux GOARCH=$(go env GOARCH) CGO_ENABLED=0 go build -o deploy/docker/api-gateway-linux ./cmd/microservices/api-gateway
Host port Service
18080 / 19090 monolith HTTP / gRPC
28080 api-gateway
18000 / 18001 h5-demo (monolith / gateway view)
18545 Anvil — chain 31337, DemoNFT + ContentRegistry
15432 / 16379 / 19000-19001 / 14222 / 28500 PostgreSQL / Redis / MinIO / NATS / Consul

scripts/docker-deploy.sh seeds the demo gating rule (content_id='demo'). A host-run acceptance needs GRPC_TARGET=localhost:19090 — the script default monolith:9090 only resolves inside the compose network.

Layout

Path What it is
cmd/monolith/ monolith entry — every plugin in one process (port 8080)
cmd/microservices/ 9 standalone entries: api-gateway, auth, transcoder, streaming, upload, metadata, cache, worker, monitor
pkg/core/ microkernel, GenericPlugin, config, event bus, logger
pkg/plugins/ 9 thin plugin wrappers — delegate to pkg/service/
pkg/service/ business logic; subpackages content/ transcoding/ streamingsvc/ gating/ upload/ category/ playbackstats/ sigchain/
pkg/gateway/ REST handlers, one file per domain (<domain>_handlers.go); routes.go is the single registration point
pkg/middleware/ auth, nft_gate, playback_token_auth, cors, circuitbreaker, ratelimit, tracing
pkg/web3/ signature, nft/ verifier, contract/, solana/, reorg, gas, events
pkg/storage/ PostgreSQL, Redis, MinIO/S3, NATS, cache
test/ unit, integration, e2e, benchmark, security
skills/ tool-neutral AI skills — catalog in skills/INDEX.md
migrations/ 42 up / 26 down SQL migrations
h5-demo/, examples/ frontend demo and standalone samples — not part of the Go build

Component Status

Ready means tested and wired into the running system; everything else — auth, NFT verification, gating, streaming, transcoding, upload, worker, storage — is Ready (full table in README.md → Product Roadmap). These two are not:

Component Status Notes
pkg/web3/account_abstraction.go Unused IAccount / UserOperation / AAProvider exist, including entry-point validation and nonce expiry, but nothing constructs them. ERC-4337 is out of scope — see TRANSITION_PLAN.md appendix D
pkg/web3/contract/registry.go (SmartContractRegistry) Unused complete in-memory registry API (register, get by name / address / chain) but never constructed outside tests. Contains no TODO markers

Conventions

Code
  • Business logic lives in pkg/service/, never in plugins. Plugins are thin wrappers over core.GenericPlugin plus a server struct.
  • Service names are lowercase-with-dashes (api-gateway, transcoder).
  • Comments: none by default. Required only for security-sensitive operations (signature verification, replay protection, block-tag safety), complex regex, or third-party workarounds.
  • Linter .golangci.yml: govet, staticcheck, errcheck, gocritic, misspell, gocyclo (max 35).
Testing
  • testify for assertions, uber/mock for mocks — run go generate ./... after changing an interface.
  • Table-driven preferred (see pkg/service/auth_wallet_test.go). Names: Test<Function>_<Scenario>. -race always on.
  • Categories: unit (no tag) · integration (PostgreSQL + Redis; CI provides them) · anvil tag (Foundry) · testnet tag (SEPOLIA_RPC).
  • Coverage floor 60%, enforced in CI.
Dependencies
  • Ask before adding one; record every change in DEPENDENCY_APPROVAL.md. Prefer stdlib. Gin is already in use.
Git
  • type(scope): description — feat | fix | refactor | test | docs | chore. One logical change per commit.
  • No force-push to shared branches. A PR must pass build + lint + test.
  • Push only when asked — each push triggers CI, and rapid pushes cancel the previous run.
Documentation
  • TRANSITION_PLAN.md is the only plan document. Never create process documents — no review reports, handovers, status files or dated scratch .md. Durable conclusions go there.
  • Where content belongs: agent knowledge → this file / AGENTS.md; long-term recall → memory/; reusable procedures → skills/; evidence → a CI run id or commit hash; dependency changes → DEPENDENCY_APPROVAL.md; job material → docs/resume/.
  • Reference docs live under docs/ — the map is docs/README.md. Superseded material goes to docs/archive/ (frozen, not maintained); never add files at docs/ root except README.md and ARCHITECTURE.md.
  • Clean up generated artifacts when done: bin/, out/, cache/, target/, coverage.out, deploy/docker/*-linux, scripts/h5-demo-acceptance/reports/. Do not delete .workbuddy/.
  • Before deleting or renaming a document, grep -rn "<path>" --include="*.md" . and repoint inbound links.

Task Completion Checklist

[ ] go build ./cmd/monolith/streamgate   # fast compile check only
[ ] make fmt && make lint
[ ] make test          # pre-existing failures: document, do not chase
[ ] make test-anvil    # if touching pkg/web3/
[ ] go mod tidy

Authoritative gate: CI (ci.yml + build.yml) — it compiles, lints, tests and Trivy-scans inside containers. A green host run is a pre-check, not acceptance.

Known Pitfalls

Symptom Cause Fix
make test fails on DB connection PostgreSQL not running docker-compose up -d postgres redis
make test-anvil fails Foundry not installed brew install foundry or foundryup
make lint fails on lines you didn't touch pre-existing baseline fix only your changed lines
NFT verify returns a wrong result using the latest block tag use safe / finalized
go-ethereum RPC timeout no RPC, or rate limited set WEB3_ETHEREUM_RPC in .env
go mod tidy drops needed packages stale go.sum go mod download && go mod tidy
Docker build fails BuildKit disabled DOCKER_BUILDKIT=1
Host golangci-lint refuses the module host binary built with Go ≤ 1.25 use a v2.12+ build, or let CI lint
Microservice events error outside Docker the Dockerfiles pass -tags nats_integration; a host make build-all does not, so NATSEventBus is the stub run the service from its image
make fullchain-deploy fails: "/deploy/docker/streamgate-linux": not found the quick Dockerfiles COPY prebuilt Linux binaries; the deploy script does not build them build deploy/docker/{streamgate,api-gateway}-linux for linux/$(go env GOARCH) first
Monolith/api-gateway exit at startup: runtime code hash mismatch solc embeds forge's absolute remappings in the metadata hash, so host and /contracts builds differ keep bytecode_hash = "none" in contracts/foundry.toml; after contract changes regenerate the record (make contracts-manifest-anvil, or point scripts/registry-manifest.sh --rpc/--address at the running stack)
make verify-docker image build fails: gnutls_handshake() failed fetching github.com Dockerfile defaults GOPROXY=direct VERIFY_GOPROXY=https://goproxy.cn,direct make verify-docker
make fullchain-test fails all four gRPC checks (exit status 1) default GRPC_TARGET=monolith:9090 is compose-internal GRPC_TARGET=localhost:19090 make fullchain-test
Image build stalls or OOMs on an 8GB Docker VM compose builds 6+ go build -a in parallel build serially: for s in auth cache metadata monitor streaming transcoder upload worker; do docker compose -f docker-compose.fullchain.yml build $s; done

Anti-Patterns

  • NEVER skip the NFT ownership check before delivering an HLS manifest.
  • NEVER expose raw video segments without authentication.
  • NEVER use a single RPC endpoint — always a fallback chain.
  • NEVER introduce speculative features or premature abstractions.
  • NEVER take the authorization subject (contract, token id, chain) from the request — it comes from server-side content_gating_rules only.

Key Design Decisions

  1. Monolith-first — one process for development; split into microservices only when scale requires it.
  2. Plugins are thin wrappers over pkg/service/, which holds the logic.
  3. Redis Lua for challenge consumption — atomic, so a challenge cannot be replayed (TOCTOU).
  4. safe/finalized block tags, never latest — a reorg must not grant access.
  5. The gate fails closed — no rules → 403, resolver error → 500, gate disabled → 503.
  6. Playback tokens are wallet-bound capabilities, carrying an explicit token_type claim and minted only after the gate passes. HLS.js cannot send Authorization on media requests, so the token travels in the query string — middleware validates it cryptographically; its mere presence never grants access.

For agents

StreamGate — Token-Gated Video Streaming for Web3

Token-gated video delivery. Wallet sign-in, server-side NFT rules, and authenticated HLS delivery.

English: Overview · Quick Start · API Docs

中文: 一句话介绍 · 快速开始

[源文档图片：Go Version；本页不自动请求远程图片] [源文档图片：License；本页不自动请求远程图片] [源文档图片：PRs Welcome；本页不自动请求远程图片] [源文档图片：Docker；本页不自动请求远程图片] [源文档图片：OpenAPI；本页不自动请求远程图片] [源文档图片：Status；本页不自动请求远程图片] [源文档图片：Discord；本页不自动请求远程图片]

Quick Start

The development stack requires Docker Compose and the Linux binaries deploy/docker/streamgate-linux and deploy/docker/api-gateway-linux, built for the Docker daemon's architecture. Prepare them with the commands in the development guide before running the target below; fullchain-deploy does not compile these binaries. First image builds and contract compilation can take several minutes. On a constrained Mac, follow the guide's serial image-build procedure to limit CPU and memory use.

make fullchain-deploy             # Postgres + Redis + MinIO + NATS + Anvil(+DemoNFT) + monolith + microservices + h5-demo
open http://localhost:18000       # connect wallet → sign in → mint DemoNFT → verify → play

Confirm the gate is actually closed before you authenticate:

curl -i http://localhost:18080/api/v1/streaming/demo/manifest.m3u8    # → 401 Unauthorized

For API-level work with no chain involved, make demo starts Postgres + Redis + MinIO and runs the monolith on :8080 (h5-demo then served at http://localhost:8080/demo). See DEPLOY.md.

That is the entire loop: wallet sign-in → on-chain NFT ownership check → protected HLS playback. make fullchain-deploy starts Anvil and deploys DemoNFT for you — no chain setup needed to try it.

The accepted candidate’s prepared local demo was independently started and exercised in both modes. Its replay commands and exact local artifact paths are in TRANSITION_PLAN.md §8.86; fresh checkouts still need the setup above. The measured 57.14-second warm startup excludes first downloads and source/submodule preparation.

Acceptance Evidence

The acceptance runner creates its own isolated stack from a clean checkout. It requires Git, Make, and Docker; host Go/Foundry/Node toolchains are unnecessary for this runner. Its results below do not establish a cold-start time for the separate development helper above.

git submodule update --init --recursive
make verify-docker VERIFY_STAGE=all     # unit · chain · durability · ops · faults · media · recovery · perf · release
make verify-docker VERIFY_STAGE=media   # or one stage at a time (each stage log lands in .verify-artifacts/)

Architecture
flowchart LR
  U["h5-demo (browser)<br/>hls.js"] -->|"1. SIWE / EIP-191 signature"| GW["API Gateway"]
  U -->|"2. manifest + .ts<br/>carrying playback_token"| GW
  GW --> AUTH["Auth<br/>challenge → JWT"]
  GW --> GATE["NFT gate middleware<br/>FAIL-CLOSED"]
  GATE -->|"ownerOf / balanceOf<br/>pinned to one block<br/>(number + hash)"| RPC[("EVM RPC<br/>+ fallback chain")]
  GATE --> RULES[("content_gating_rules<br/>server-side only")]
  GW --> STREAM["Streaming<br/>manifest + per-user token"]
  STREAM --> OBJ[("Object storage<br/>HLS segments")]
  UP["Upload"] --> Q[["NATS JetStream + DLQ"]]
  Q --> TR["Transcoder worker<br/>FFmpeg ABR ladder"] --> OBJ
  RPC --> REG["ContentRegistry<br/>on-chain content log"]

Two properties worth knowing before you read the code:

  • Authorization subject is server-side only. The NFT contract, token id and chain come from content_gating_rules via RuleResolver — never from request query parameters.
  • The gate fails closed. No rules → 403 GATING_REQUIRED; rule resolution error → 500; gate disabled → 503. The permanent regression tests live in pkg/gateway/authz_regression_test.go.

💡 The Problem

Content creators lose revenue to piracy. Access control is usually one of:

  • Passwords (shared, stolen, phished)
  • Manual whitelists (doesn't scale, no transparency)
  • Endpoint DRM (requires a supported client playback integration)

StreamGate replaces the first two with on-chain NFT ownership: hold the token, watch the video. Content owners configure server-side rules; the service checks possession on chain.

Scope note — this is access authorization, not DRM. DRM protects content against the endpoint (hardware trust root, keys that never leave the trusted execution environment). StreamGate's HLS delivery uses short-lived, content-bound playback tokens, which reject unauthenticated media requests. An authorized viewer can still copy a valid bearer token during its lifetime or capture playback. It is not a DRM substitute; see What StreamGate Is Not.

🎯 一句话介绍

持有特定 NFT 才能观看视频的内容分发平台

用户持有 NFT → 验证所有权 → 获得观看权限 → 播放视频

📖 四个核心环节

1. 钱包签名登录
  • 用户不需要密码，通过钱包签名登录
  • 服务端做 EIP-191 验签、nonce、过期校验和防重放（challenge 用 Redis Lua 原子消费）
2. NFT 所有权校验
  • 服务端通过真实链上调用校验持有权（ownerOf/balanceOf，ERC-721/1155，ERC-165 自动探测）
  • 只有持有 NFT，才允许访问受保护内容
  • 读链固定用 safe block tag；检测到重组自动失效缓存；正结果缓存 TTL 上限 15 秒
3. 受保护的 HLS 投递
  • 通过校验后才下发 manifest；分片用内容绑定、短 TTL 的 playback token 投递
  • 令牌绑定钱包与客户端指纹，跨用户复用会被拒绝；链上 RPC 不进播放热路径
4. 转码 Worker
  • 视频上传后通过 FFmpeg 转码为 HLS（4 档 ABR 阶梯 + 实时进度解析）
  • 任务经 NATS JetStream 分发，带重试、超时与卡死 worker 回收，失败任务进 DLQ
主链路
钱包签名登录 -> NFT 所有权校验 -> 放行 manifest -> 播放 HLS 视频

📖 Project Overview

StreamGate is a Go-based NFT-gated streaming backend. It combines a video distribution pipeline you would expect in a media backend with Web3 capabilities such as wallet sign-in, NFT ownership verification, and RPC reliability handling.

🎯 Why StreamGate?

Token-based access replaces passwords and manual whitelists.

Problem Traditional approach StreamGate
Password sharing Rate limiting, MFA Access requires a wallet signature — no shared secret to leak. Sharing the wallet itself still works; the platform rate-limits per wallet.
Manual access management Admin dashboards, CSV exports Own the NFT → access is derived from on-chain state, no per-user admin work
Cross-platform Multiple SDKs per platform One API — REST/gRPC/OpenAPI

The entitlement gate does not control an authorized client's copy of the media. Client playback protection is outside the implemented scope.

🚫 What StreamGate Is Not
Not this Because
Decentralized video network StreamGate is self-hosted infrastructure, not a peer-to-peer network. For decentralized transcoding, see Livepeer.
Traditional DRM (Widevine/FairPlay) StreamGate checks server-side entitlement; endpoint DRM integration is outside its implemented scope.
Managed SaaS / Cloud Community edition is self-hosted. A hosted version may be added in a future release.
No-code platform StreamGate is API-first. A Web UI is planned but not yet available.
👥 Who Is It For?
Role Goal Quick Start
Platform Developer Integrate NFT-gated video via API Read the OpenAPI spec — no Go required
Content Creator Upload video, set NFT rules, go live Use make demo (single-binary, no blockchain needed for code-level experimentation)
Node Operator Deploy and scale StreamGate Use make deploy-monolith (7 containers) or make deploy-microservices (10 service binaries, 16 containers), or K8s manifests in deploy/k8s/. See DEPLOY.md for the 5-minute guide.
📦 Product Roadmap
P0 — Core Flow (Implemented, hardening)

Core authentication, NFT verification, and streaming pipeline — implemented and covered by tests. The authorization path went through a fail-closed security audit and is now pinned by permanent regression tests (pkg/gateway/authz_regression_test.go). Known gaps are listed under Project Status.

  • [x] Wallet Sign-In — EIP-191 / EIP-712 / SIWE (EIP-4361) + Solana ed25519
  • [x] NFT Ownership Verification — ERC-721, ERC-1155, ERC-165 auto-detect
  • [x] NFTOwnershipsApproval (TOCTOU Protection) — CheckApproval + CheckApprovalAutoDetect
  • [x] HLS Streaming — Manifest generation + per-user playback tokens
  • [x] Video Upload — Chunked resumable upload with integrity checks
  • [x] FFmpeg Transcoding — Multi-profile HLS/DASH with worker pool
  • [~] Multi-chain EVM — Ethereum, Polygon, BSC, Arbitrum, Optimism with RPC failover. Acceptance is narrower than the chain list: the end-to-end evidence (real nodes, pinned block, hash re-check, endpoint failover, reorg) is run against a local Anvil chain only (test/integration/gating_anvil_test.go). The other chains are wired through the same code path but have no equivalent acceptance run.
  • [~] Solana Integration — Multi-endpoint RPC failover, Metaplex NFT. Same caveat: wired, but no end-to-end acceptance run; Solana ownership is not block-pinned (it has no EVM block semantics — ownership maps 1:1 mint→token-account).
  • [x] JWT Auth — HS256/RS256 with key rotation support
  • [x] Rate Limiting — Global + per-wallet (10 req/min)
  • [x] Configuration Management — Viper YAML + env vars with hot reload
  • [x] Graceful Shutdown — Context-aware with 30s timeout
P1 — Usability & Operations (Mostly complete)

Operations and developer experience — implemented components are listed below; production rollout is unverified. Observability is partial (metrics endpoint only, no scrape stack).

  • [x] Dual-mode Deployment — Monolith (dev) + 9 microservices (prod)
  • [x] Docker + Docker Compose — Full stack with health checks
  • [x] Kubernetes Manifests — Deployments, services, config maps, secrets
  • [~] Prometheus Metrics — /metrics endpoint (RED metrics) on every service; scrape stack (Grafana/Jaeger) removed during simplification, see docs/ARCHITECTURE.md
  • [x] gRPC API — Full proto definitions + interceptors + health protocol
  • [~] OpenTelemetry Tracing — Gin + gRPC instrumented; Jaeger export removed during simplification
  • [x] gRPC TLS — Certificate-based server-side TLS
  • [x] Structured Logging — Zap with request ID correlation
  • [x] Health Check Aggregation — /health, /ready, /health/live
  • [x] Database Migrations — 35 versioned migrations with paired rollback (19 .down.sql)
  • [x] CI/CD Pipeline — GitHub Actions: lint → test → build → security → docker (security and docker are enforcing gates; coverage floor 60%)
  • [x] Multi-stage Docker Build — Builder + distroless runtime support
  • [x] Blues/Green + Canary Deployment — K8s rollout strategies
  • [x] SLO Alerts — Prometheus alerting rules with burn rate
P2 — Ecosystem & Advanced (Planned)

Features planned for future releases.

  • [ ] Web Admin UI — Browser-based content and gating management
  • [ ] JS / Python SDK — First-party client libraries
  • [ ] Account Abstraction (ERC-4337) — Gasless wallet login
  • [ ] Social Login — Email/OAuth → embedded wallet
  • [ ] NFT-gated Live Streaming — WebRTC → HLS real-time
  • [ ] Analytics Dashboard — Viewer metrics, revenue tracking
  • [ ] IPFS Pinning Service — Decentralized content storage
  • [ ] Commercial License — Enterprise support, SLA, SSO

📊 Performance Metrics

These are design targets, not measured results. The table describes the shape of the design. Measured results are recorded in TRANSITION_PLAN.md §8.16: fixed-budget local runs (1/10/50/100 concurrent viewers, fault-injection matrix, p95 regression ratio) and the CI perf-harness gate. No production capacity or soak test has been run.

Metric Design target
API response time (P95) < 200ms
Video playback startup < 2 seconds
Concurrent users 10,000+
Cache hit rate > 80%
Service availability > 99.9%
RPC uptime > 99.5%
Transaction confirmation < 2 minutes
Monitoring & Observability

Prometheus Metrics (http://localhost:9090)

  • HTTP request count and latency
  • Cache hit/miss rate
  • Transcoding task status and duration
  • NFT verification request count
  • System resource usage (CPU, memory, disk)
  • Service health status

Jaeger Tracing (http://localhost:16686)

  • Distributed request tracing
  • Service dependency visualization
  • Performance bottleneck identification
  • Error tracking

Consul UI (http://localhost:8500)

  • Service registration status
  • Health check results
  • Service discovery
  • Key-value store

Grafana Dashboards (http://localhost:3000)

  • Real-time metrics visualization
  • Custom alerts
  • Performance trends
  • Resource utilization

📈 Project Status

Core flow (wallet sign-in → NFT ownership check → protected HLS playback) is implemented end-to-end and pinned by regression tests. The authorization path was audited and reworked to fail closed. CI evidence is tied to the exact candidate listed above.

Current Priorities
Priority Focus Status
P0 Wallet sign-in + NFT verification + protected HLS access Implemented, tested
P1 Redis cache + RPC failover + metrics Implemented
P1 Worker + FFmpeg pipeline Implemented
P2 Further microservice split and polish Deferred — not a current proof point
Known Limitations
Area Current state
Account abstraction (ERC-4337) Not implemented — pkg/web3/account_abstraction.go returns an explicit error
Observability stack /metrics (RED) on every service; the Grafana/Jaeger scrape stack was removed during simplification
IPFS Not wired into the upload path
Decentralization Self-hosted infrastructure, not a peer-to-peer network — ownership checks read the chain, the platform itself is centralized
Load testing No production capacity or soak test has been run; measured fixed-budget local runs are recorded in TRANSITION_PLAN.md §8 with their candidate identities

See TRANSITION_PLAN.md for the engineering plan and docs/ARCHITECTURE.md for the architecture.

Example Unit Test
package auth_test

import (
    "context"
    "testing"
    "streamgate/pkg/service"
    "streamgate/test/helpers"
)

func TestAuthService_Login(t *testing.T) {
    // Setup
    ctx := context.Background()
    db := helpers.SetupTestDB()
    defer db.Close()
    
    authService := service.NewAuthService(db)
    
    // Test
    user, err := authService.Login(ctx, "0x742d35Cc6634C0532925a3b844Bc9e7595f42bE")
    
    // Assert
    helpers.AssertNoError(t, err)
    helpers.AssertNotNil(t, user)
    helpers.AssertEqual(t, user.WalletAddress, "0x742d35Cc6634C0532925a3b844Bc9e7595f42bE")
}

func TestAuthService_VerifySignature(t *testing.T) {
    tests := []struct {
        name      string
        signature string
        nonce     string
        expected  bool
    }{
        {
            name:      "valid signature",
            signature: "0x...",
            nonce:     "streamgate_nonce_123",
            expected:  true,
        },
        {
            name:      "invalid signature",
            signature: "0xinvalid",
            nonce:     "streamgate_nonce_123",
            expected:  false,
        },
    }
    
    for _, tt := range tests {
        t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
            result := service.VerifySignature(tt.signature, tt.nonce)
            helpers.AssertEqual(t, result, tt.expected)
        })
    }
}
Running Unit Tests
# Run all unit tests
make test

# Run specific test
go test ./test/unit/service/...

# Run with coverage
go test -cover ./test/unit/...

# Run with verbose output
go test -v ./test/unit/...

# Run with race detector
go test -race ./test/unit/...

Example Integration Test
package auth_test

import (
    "context"
    "testing"
    "streamgate/pkg/service"
    "streamgate/test/helpers"
)

func TestAuthFlow_Complete(t *testing.T) {
    // Setup
    ctx := context.Background()
    db := helpers.SetupTestDB()
    cache := helpers.SetupTestRedis()
    defer db.Close()
    defer cache.Close()
    
    authService := service.NewAuthService(db, cache)
    
    // Step 1: Get nonce
    nonce, err := authService.GetNonce(ctx, "0x742d35Cc6634C0532925a3b844Bc9e7595f42bE")
    helpers.AssertNoError(t, err)
    helpers.AssertNotEmpty(t, nonce)
    
    // Step 2: Sign nonce (simulated)
    signature := helpers.SignMessage(nonce)
    
    // Step 3: Verify signature
    token, err := authService.VerifyAndGetToken(ctx, "0x742d35Cc6634C0532925a3b844Bc9e7595f42bE", signature, nonce)
    helpers.AssertNoError(t, err)
    helpers.AssertNotEmpty(t, token)
    
    // Step 4: Verify token
    claims, err := authService.VerifyToken(ctx, token)
    helpers.AssertNoError(t, err)
    helpers.AssertEqual(t, claims.WalletAddress, "0x742d35Cc6634C0532925a3b844Bc9e7595f42bE")
}
Running Integration Tests
# Run all integration tests
go test ./test/integration/...

# Run specific integration test
go test ./test/integration/auth/...

# Run with coverage
go test -cover ./test/integration/...

# Run with timeout
go test -timeout 5m ./test/integration/...

Example E2E Test
package e2e_test

import (
    "context"
    "testing"
    "time"
    "streamgate/test/helpers"
)

func TestUploadAndStreamingFlow(t *testing.T) {
    // Setup
    ctx := context.Background()
    client := helpers.SetupTestClient()
    defer client.Close()
    
    // Step 1: Authenticate
    token, err := client.Authenticate(ctx, "0x742d35Cc6634C0532925a3b844Bc9e7595f42bE")
    helpers.AssertNoError(t, err)
    
    // Step 2: Create content
    content, err := client.CreateContent(ctx, token, &CreateContentRequest{
        Title:       "Test Video",
        Description: "Test video for E2E testing",
    })
    helpers.AssertNoError(t, err)
    helpers.AssertNotNil(t, content)
    
    // Step 3: Upload file
    uploadID, err := client.InitUpload(ctx, token, &InitUploadRequest{
        Filename:    "test.mp4",
        Size:        1073741824,
        ContentType: "video/mp4",
    })
    helpers.AssertNoError(t, err)
    
    // Upload chunks
    for i := 1; i <= 205; i++ {
        err := client.UploadChunk(ctx, token, uploadID, i, getChunkData(i))
        helpers.AssertNoError(t, err)
    }
    
    // Complete upload
    err = client.CompleteUpload(ctx, token, uploadID, content.ID)
    helpers.AssertNoError(t, err)
    
    // Step 4: Wait for transcoding
    time.Sleep(5 * time.Second)
    
    // Step 5: Get streaming manifest
    manifest, err := client.GetManifest(ctx, token, content.ID)
    helpers.AssertNoError(t, err)
    helpers.AssertNotEmpty(t, manifest)
    
    // Step 6: Get segment
    segment, err := client.GetSegment(ctx, token, content.ID, 1)
    helpers.AssertNoError(t, err)
    helpers.AssertNotEmpty(t, segment)
}
Running E2E Tests

# Run with timeout
go test -timeout 10m ./test/e2e/...

# Run in parallel
go test -parallel 4 ./test/e2e/...

package benchmark_test

import (
    "context"
    "testing"
    "streamgate/pkg/service"
    "streamgate/test/helpers"
)

func BenchmarkAuthService_Login(b *testing.B) {
    ctx := context.Background()
    db := helpers.SetupTestDB()
    defer db.Close()
    
    authService := service.NewAuthService(db)
    
    b.ResetTimer()
    for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
        authService.Login(ctx, "0x742d35Cc6634C0532925a3b844Bc9e7595f42bE")
    }
}

func BenchmarkContentService_Query(b *testing.B) {
    ctx := context.Background()
    db := helpers.SetupTestDB()
    defer db.Close()
    
    contentService := service.NewContentService(db)
    
    b.ResetTimer()
    for i := 0; i < b.N; i++ {
        contentService.ListContent(ctx, 1, 20)
    }
}

Test Utilities

Setup Helpers
// test/helpers/setup.go

// SetupTestDB creates a test database
func SetupTestDB() *sql.DB {
    db, _ := sql.Open("postgres", "postgres://test:test@localhost/streamgate_test")
    // Run migrations
    return db
}

// SetupTestRedis creates a test Redis connection
func SetupTestRedis() *redis.Client {
    return redis.NewClient(&redis.Options{
        Addr: "localhost:6379",
        DB:   1, // Use separate DB for tests
    })
}

// SetupTestStorage creates a test MinIO connection
func SetupTestStorage() *minio.Client {
    client, _ := minio.New("localhost:9000", &minio.Options{
        // Local test creds come from env, not committed literals.
		Creds: credentials.NewStaticV4(os.Getenv("STREAMGATE_STORAGE_ACCESS_KEY"), os.Getenv("STREAMGATE_STORAGE_SECRET_KEY"), ""),
    })
    return client
}
Assertion Helpers
// test/helpers/assert.go

func AssertNoError(t *testing.T, err error) {
    if err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("expected no error, got %v", err)
    }
}

func AssertEqual(t *testing.T, got, expected interface{}) {
    if got != expected {
        t.Fatalf("expected %v, got %v", expected, got)
    }
}

func AssertNotNil(t *testing.T, value interface{}) {
    if value == nil {
        t.Fatal("expected non-nil value")
    }
}

func AssertNotEmpty(t *testing.T, value string) {
    if value == "" {
        t.Fatal("expected non-empty string")
    }
}
Fixture Helpers
// test/helpers/fixtures.go

func LoadFixture(name string) interface{} {
    data, _ := ioutil.ReadFile(fmt.Sprintf("test/fixtures/%s.json", name))
    var result interface{}
    json.Unmarshal(data, &result)
    return result
}

func CreateTestUser() *User {
    return &User{
        ID:              "user_123",
        WalletAddress:   "0x742d35Cc6634C0532925a3b844Bc9e7595f42bE",
        CreatedAt:       time.Now(),
    }
}

func CreateTestContent() *Content {
    return &Content{
        ID:          "content_123",
        Title:       "Test Video",
        Description: "Test video",
        Status:      "ready",
        CreatedAt:   time.Now(),
    }
}

CI/CD Integration

GitHub Actions
# .github/workflows/test.yml
name: Tests

on: [push, pull_request]

jobs:
  test:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    
    services:
      postgres:
        image: postgres:15
        env:
          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: test
          POSTGRES_DB: streamgate_test
        options: >-
          --health-cmd pg_isready
          --health-interval 10s
          --health-timeout 5s
          --health-retries 5
      
      redis:
        image: redis:7
        options: >-
          --health-cmd "redis-cli ping"
          --health-interval 10s
          --health-timeout 5s
          --health-retries 5
    
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v3
      
      - uses: actions/setup-go@v4
        with:
          go-version: '1.24'
      
      - name: Run tests
        run: make test
      
      - name: Upload coverage
        uses: codecov/codecov-action@v3
        with:
          files: ./coverage.out
Running Tests Locally
# Run all tests
make test

# Run tests with coverage
make coverage

# Run tests with race detector
go test -race ./...

# Run tests in parallel
go test -parallel 4 ./...

# Run tests with timeout
go test -timeout 5m ./...

Best Practices

1. Test Organization
  • Group related tests in packages
  • Use descriptive test names
  • Follow table-driven test pattern
2. Test Isolation
  • Each test should be independent
  • Use separate databases for tests
  • Clean up resources after tests
3. Test Coverage
  • Aim for 100% coverage
  • Test happy path and error cases
  • Test edge cases
4. Test Performance
  • Keep unit tests fast (<100ms)
  • Use mocks for external dependencies
  • Run integration tests separately
5. Test Maintenance
  • Keep tests up-to-date with code
  • Refactor tests when code changes
  • Remove obsolete tests
