h5-demo-acceptance

Playwright-driven smoke test for the 5 h5-demo HTML pages. Runs after make fullchain-deploy (or any equivalent live stack) and verifies the front-end can talk to the back-end through the same flows a human would exercise.

What it runs

  1. stack pre-check (lib/stack.mjs) — 9 probes, exits early if any fail:

    • monolith /health
    • api-gateway /health
    • all 5 h5-demo HTML pages return 200
    • Anvil RPC eth_chainId
    • api-gateway routes the NFT dev-mint endpoint (expects 401 — auth middleware must be live)

  2. 5 browser specs (specs/01..05-*.mjs) — one per HTML file:

    • 01-index.mjs — full acceptance: backend URL → demo wallet → sign & login → auto-mint → verify NFT → player visible
    • 02-flow.mjs — upload UI present, MetaMask connect click handled
    • 03-debug.mjs — Run Full Auto Flow + auth + NFT (skipped gracefully if click races)
    • 04-playground.mjs — refresh Web3 status dashboard; verifies all 4 stat blocks + RPC detail + config table populate
    • 05-trace.mjs — pick scenario, run, verify middleware chain + response + timing summary populate

  3. report artefacts under reports/<timestamp>/:

    • summary.json — top-level pass/fail + per-spec durations
    • <spec>/result.json — checks + errors
    • <spec>/console.json — full page console log
    • <spec>/network.json — request/response summary
    • <spec>/<name>.png — full-page screenshots at key steps

Usage

# Run the entire suite (≈95 s on a warm stack)
make h5-demo-acceptance

# Run a single spec
make h5-demo-acceptance-spec SPEC=01-index
# or
cd scripts/h5-demo-acceptance && node run.mjs --only=02-flow

# Pin the report directory (handy for CI artefact upload)
cd scripts/h5-demo-acceptance && node run.mjs --report-dir=reports/ci-$(date +%Y%m%d)

Requirements

  • A live stack: make fullchain-deploy, or any equivalent running stack. Endpoints are resolved from the environment (below), not hardcoded.
  • Playwright Node bindings: cd scripts/h5-demo-acceptance && npm install (the committed package-lock.json is enough). Host runs only — see the Docker section.
  • Browser: PW_CHANNEL='chrome' (default) uses the host's Google Chrome and avoids a Playwright chromium download. Inside a container set PW_CHANNEL= to use the bundled Chromium instead.
Endpoints (env-driven)

lib/urls.mjs is the single place endpoints are resolved, so the same specs run on the host and inside a container with no edits:

Env var Host default Inside the compose network
H5_BASE http://localhost:18000 http://h5-demo:8080
API_BASE http://localhost:28080 http://api-gateway:8080
MONOLITH_BASE http://localhost:18080 http://monolith:8080
ANVIL_BASE http://localhost:18545 http://anvil:8545
Docker (preferred)

Per the project's rule that build/run/acceptance happen in Docker, run both the stack and the browser in containers:

~/.workbuddy/skills/streamgate-docker-acceptance/scripts/docker-acceptance.sh --repo <repo-root>

That script copies this suite into a Playwright container attached to the compose network and points the env vars above at the service names. Do not npm install on the host in order to run in Docker — the image is Linux, and installing over the host's node_modules replaces the macOS Playwright binaries and breaks later host runs.

What is filtered as transient

The console + network probes record everything to console.json / network.json, but the following are recorded, not failed:

  • 503 on /health during the first probe (cold-start race)
  • 404 on /api/v1/streaming/<id>/manifest.m3u8 (no real video uploaded in the smoke test)
  • favicon.ico 404s

If any of those patterns change in the real app, the filters here should be updated in lib/common.mjs.

What a pass does not prove

Read this before reporting a green run as more than it is:

  • Dependency health is now enforced. The monolith-health and api-gateway-health probes require status == "healthy" in the body, so a 503 {"status":"unhealthy"} fails the pre-check and aborts the run. A 503 console error from /health is also no longer filtered as transient (lib/common.mjs) — only /health is exempt from the 503 filter, and no longer.
  • No media pipeline coverage. Nothing is uploaded or transcoded, so HLS manifest generation and segment delivery are untested (404 on manifests is expected and filtered).
  • The demo-wallet path is exercised, not a real injected wallet extension.
  • 03-debug reports click raced — non-blocking when the auto-flow buttons lose a race; those checks are recorded but never fail the spec.

When the pre-check fails on health, read the detail rather than the summary — it carries both numbers, e.g. http=503 body.status=unhealthy expected=healthy.

To inspect a dependency directly:

curl -s http://localhost:18080/health | jq -r '.status, (.checks | to_entries[] | "\(.key)=\(.value.status)")'

Exit codes

  • 0 — every probe and every spec passed
  • 1 — at least one probe or spec failed (stack pre-check or browser check); the failing checks are listed in stdout and persisted under reports/<timestamp>/

The script writes a final verdict line (PASSED / FAILED) and a summary.json so CI can gate on either.

StreamGate 项目讲述稿 + 演示 + 追问（SG-09 / M6 的 AI 交付部分）

当前工程交付（2026-11-02）：PASS WITH BOUNDARIES。 候选 d0f05152edc1fb404cb2d78b734c95244e1a7a4e 完整 Docker all/M0～M8 原始退出 0；CI run 41343759379 attempt 3 为 55/55 success，含 evidence/Publishable。本机已准备 demo 从空演示卷 57.14s 就绪，两模式复验各 77/77。证据见 TRANSITION_PLAN.md §8.86；其余带旧 SHA 的数字仍为历史。本材料提交不继承代码候选 CI。本人履历/贡献确认、讲述/录屏/投递仍待本人，生产与 testnet 未验证。

纪律：每个数字均须指向 SHA / 测试名 / 实测断言；历史代码入口按对应 SHA 解读，当前交付绑定 d0f05152，其余数字按逐项注明的历史 SHA 解读；证据见 TRANSITION_PLAN.md §8.86，行号仅用于导航。 诚实边界：不说 DRM、不说完全去中心化 / 无需信任、不说生产高可用已验证、不说版权确权；多 RPC 回退 ≠ 轻客户端证明；NFT 持有 ≠ 媒体版权。 AI 归属：项目由 AI 辅助编码。AI 的代码生成不等于"我独立设计实现"——个人贡献主张逐条标【待本人确认】（第六节）。 下文中 ✅ 与测量值描述其对应 SHA 上的历史证据，不代表当前候选整体通过；当前工程交付以 TRANSITION_PLAN.md 顶部标记与 §8.86 为准。⏳ = 当前待验收 / 待实测；【待本人确认】= 需本人核实。ChainPulse 是独立项目，证据另行整理，不用 StreamGate 的验收为它背书。

一、3 分钟自我介绍（口播稿，约 700 字）

"我是 [姓名]，10 年以上 C++，其中 6 年多在音视频流媒体——iOS / Android 原生播放器和流媒体后端，现在转 Web3 后端方向。展示项目是 StreamGate：NFT 门控的流媒体后端，业务问题是'内容会员制'——链上资产决定观看权限，媒体交付仍是真实的流媒体系统。

主线可以现场复现：真实钱包签名登录 → 一次性挑战防重放 → 服务端 NFT 规则 → 同链同区块持仓证据 → 60 秒播放能力 → 真实 HLS 解码播放 → NFT 转移后权限失效 → RPC / Redis 故障下的拒绝与恢复。

三个最硬的证据：

第一，签名与授权。challenge 是 128 位 nonce、Redis Lua 一次性原子消费，重放必拒；gating 规则 owner 校验在业务层、与写入同一条 SQL 语句，A/B 双钱包越权负例在真实 Postgres 上 7 例全跑 0 skip；服务端代签只有 development + 31337 + 显式开关三重门才存在。

第二，链状态与撤销。授权读取固定到区块号与哈希，正证据总年龄 ≤5 秒。能力 TTL 60 秒 + 证据 5 秒 + 容差 5 秒，推导出 safe 视图确认转移后 70 秒内拒绝的上界。当前候选 d0f05152 两条部署腿的 HTTP/gRPC 实测分别为 61.799s/62.403s 与 59.732s/60.193s；链最终性等待不包含在这些计时中。历史 67 秒只是能力自然过期，不作为转移撤销证明。

第三，媒体交付。原件由服务端算 SHA-256 封存进不可覆盖的 key；ffprobe 白名单门拒绝超限而不是截断；转码跑在独立 worker 容器（非 root、无 JWT、无链私钥），验收用数据库 lease_owner 加容器日志做执行归因；播放列表用 FFmpeg 真实 EXTINF，只重写 URI。当前候选 d0f05152 两条部署腿各 77/77，通过真实解码与浏览器播放；同 SHA CI run 41343759379 为 55/55 success。本机 warm demo 57.14s，另有两腿各 77/77 真实复验；仅为本次 Mac 环境记录。

关于 AI：项目是 AI 辅助编码的，我的工作是设计决策、验收判据、代码评审和故障定位——三次'真跑一次'才暴露的问题：分片忽略 Range、规则缓存 30 秒不生效、三个 nonce 分配器在真实栈上互相顶掉交易。AI 让写代码变便宜，让判断代码对不对变贵，这个项目给我的都是判断侧的训练。

边界也说清楚：这是'链上权限 + 平台媒体服务'，RPC provider 是信任前提不是密码学证明；NFT 持有不等于版权；它不是 DRM。"

二、10 分钟演示脚本（登录 → 播放 → 转移 → 拒绝 → 故障恢复）

前置（不计入 10 分钟）
make fullchain-deploy                          # PG+Redis+MinIO+NATS+Anvil+monolith+worker+h5
curl -s http://localhost:18080/health          # {"status":"healthy"}
bash scripts/fullchain-acceptance.sh http://localhost:18080 | tee /tmp/acc.log   # 可选，66 项留档

变量（演示前 export；是运行时数据，背"从哪里取"不背值）：

BASE=http://localhost:18080
ANVIL=http://localhost:18545                   # chainId 31337
KEY_A=0xac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80   # 持有人（Anvil 确定性账户）
KEY_B=0x59c6995e998f97a5a0044966f0945389dc9e86dae88c7a8412f4603b6b78690d   # 无 NFT 钱包
WALLET_A=$(cast wallet address --private-key $KEY_A); WALLET_B=$(cast wallet address --private-key $KEY_B)
CONTENT_ID=$(docker exec sg-fc-postgres psql -U postgres -d streamgate -tAc "SELECT id FROM contents ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1")
RULE_ID=$(docker exec sg-fc-postgres psql -U postgres -d streamgate -tAc "SELECT id FROM content_gating_rules ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1")
DEMO_NFT=$(docker exec sg-fc-postgres psql -U postgres -d streamgate -tAc "SELECT contract_address FROM content_gating_rules ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1")
TOKEN_ID=<验收输出 / 链上事件里查到的持有人 token id>
阶段 1：真实登录（2 分钟）

H5 页 http://localhost:18000/demo/ 连接 demo 钱包签名登录，或走与验收相同的 CLI 路径：

CH=$(curl -s -X POST $BASE/api/v1/auth/challenge -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "{\"wallet\":\"$WALLET_A\",\"chain_id\":31337}")
MSG=$(printf '%s' "$CH" | python3 -c 'import sys,json;print(json.load(sys.stdin)["message"])')
CID=$(printf '%s' "$CH" | python3 -c 'import sys,json;print(json.load(sys.stdin)["challenge_id"])')
SIG=$(cast wallet sign --private-key $KEY_A "$MSG")
TOKEN_A=$(curl -s -X POST $BASE/api/v1/auth/login -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d "{\"wallet\":\"$WALLET_A\",\"chain_id\":31337,\"challenge_id\":\"$CID\",\"signature\":\"$SIG\"}" \
  | python3 -c 'import sys,json;print(json.load(sys.stdin)["token"])')

预期断言：Wallet A challenge -> personal_sign -> login issued a token、Forged HS256 token rejected (401)。 讲一句：nonce 128 位、TTL ≤300s、同一 challenge 第二次消费必失败（MarkChallengeUsed 是 Redis Lua 单命令原子操作）。

阶段 2：真实播放（2 分钟）
curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN_A" \
  $BASE/api/v1/streaming/$CONTENT_ID/manifest.m3u8      # → 200
# 浏览器 http://localhost:18000/demo/ 真播

预期断言：Wallet A (holder) receives the gated manifest (200)、Downloaded N segment(s)、All segments fully decode with FFmpeg、Range request returns 206 + Content-Range、Playlist actual timing: … summing to 10.0s、Browser played the stream and time advanced（hls.js 真解码，currentTime ≥3s，实测 3.51–3.75s）。 讲一句：播放列表是 FFmpeg 真实产物只重写 URI；Range 是实测发现修出来的缺陷（修复前恒 200 + 整段）。

阶段 3：NFT 转移 → 权限失效（2 分钟）
cast send $DEMO_NFT "transferFrom(address,address,uint256)" $WALLET_A $WALLET_B $TOKEN_ID --rpc-url $ANVIL --private-key $KEY_A
cast rpc anvil_mine 48 --rpc-url $ANVIL     # 推进 safe 视图（Anvil 实测滞后约 32 块；无流量不前进）
curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN_A" \
  $BASE/api/v1/streaming/$CONTENT_ID/manifest.m3u8      # → 403
# 转回必须用 B 的私钥（调用方不是持有人会 revert）
cast send $DEMO_NFT "transferFrom(address,address,uint256)" $WALLET_B $WALLET_A $TOKEN_ID --rpc-url $ANVIL --private-key $KEY_B
cast rpc anvil_mine 48 --rpc-url $ANVIL     # 复验 → 200

预期断言：Transferred the holder's N token(s) away (balance now 0)、The safe view now includes the transfer、Transferring the holding away denies the next manifest (403)、Transferring the holding back restores access (200)。 讲一句：gate 读 safe 视图——safe 没越过转移块时 200 是正确行为，所以验收先断言余额归零、显式挖块、断言 safe 越块，最后才判 403。

阶段 4：拒绝矩阵（2 分钟）
TOKEN_B=<按阶段 1 同法用 KEY_B 登录>
curl -s -o /tmp/b.json -w '%{http_code}\n' -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN_B" \
  $BASE/api/v1/streaming/$CONTENT_ID/manifest.m3u8      # → 403，/tmp/b.json 无 #EXTM3U
# 无 token → 401；capability 当 Bearer 打管理 API → 401；跨内容 capability → 401

预期断言：Wallet B (no NFT) refused the manifest (403)、Wallet B's refusal carried no media、Unauthenticated request rejected (401)、A capability replayed as a Bearer token on a management API is refused (401)、Playback token refused for another content、Path traversal in the segment name refused。 讲一句：fail-closed——零规则 403、依赖不可核验 503、未注册代签路由 404（不是 401/403，避免暴露"存在一个受保护的签名器"）。

阶段 5：故障与恢复（2 分钟）
bash scripts/dependency-fault-acceptance.sh    # 本机实测 ALL 16 PASSED（脚本自停/起依赖并恢复）

预期：RPC 全断 → 新 manifest 503 NFT_VERIFY_ERROR，已发能力的分片仍 200 且仍按原有效期到期（链不可达不能延长它），恢复后新 manifest 200；Redis 断 → 受保护资源 401、/health 仍 200，恢复后 200。 可选：docker kill sg-fc-transcoder && docker start sg-fc-transcoder 模拟 worker 崩溃——证据是测试 TestAcceptance_TranscodeRedeliveryAndCrash（lease 过期回 pending、重投递不重复副作用）；"≤60s 重新认领"是 SG-14 SLO，⏳ 未实测，不报数。

三、30 分钟深入讲述（1 + 4 + 1）

0. 开场（2min）：用第一节压缩版——业务问题 → 主线 → AI 一句话 → 边界一句话。

1. 签名与资源授权（7min）

  • 流程：challenge 下发一次性消息 → personal_sign → login 验签并原子消费 → JWT。challenge：16 字节 nonce、TTL ≤300s、绑定地址与链；消费是 Redis Lua 单命令"读 used + 写 used"——分两步有 TOCTOU，多实例下进程锁无效。
  • 验签三路径：EIP-191 / EIP-712 / SIWE(EIP-4361)——SIWE 逐字段校验 domain/URI/chain/nonce/address（pkg/web3/signature/siwe.go:ValidateSIWEMessage），地址比对 subtle.ConstantTimeCompare，合约钱包 EIP-1271，未知 signType 显式拒绝（旧实现静默落到默认 personal_sign）。
  • 凭据边界：capability 有独立 token_type/audience，JWT 中间件白名单（""/access）其余 401；算法精确固定 HS256/RS256（家族校验会放过 HS384/HS512）；iss/aud 配置即强制。capability ≠ access token，反向也不行（ParsePlaybackToken 要求自己的 audience）。
  • owner 隔离：gating-rule create/update/delete 在业务层条件写，ruleID→contentID 的定位放进同一条 UPDATE/DELETE 的 EXISTS，读写间无窗口；404/403 稳定区分；匿名 401。
  • 代签边界：通用 /web3/tx/send 已删除；存活的 /nft/dev/mint 固定合约 + 固定 selector + value 恒 0 + 铸给已认证钱包；三重门（debug + 31337 + features.demo_tx_signer）任一缺失即不注册（404）。
  • 链写 nonce：(chainID, signer) 在 pg_advisory_xact_lock 下取 max(DB+1, 链 pending)，UNIQUE(chain,signer,nonce)；先持久化 signed raw tx/nonce/hash 再广播，RPC 超时记 broadcast_unknown，恢复时查 receipt 或重播同一 raw 字节，绝不重签。
  • 双入口一致（HTTP/gRPC）：HTTP 与 gRPC 共用同一授权器与规则快照——一次读取得规则＋规则版本＋权威读取时间，能力只能由该带封印的结果签发，没有第二套授权逻辑。gRPC GetManifest/GetSegment 按真实链上数量执行 ERC-721/1155、token_id、min_balance 与 OR 规则（原先用布尔 VerifyNFTOwnership，且把能力 TTL 硬编码成 2 分钟——两者都已删）；同一请求同时带 access 与 capability 时钱包/会话/epoch 必须一致。验收第 9b 节（test/grpcparity）在两条部署腿各跑一遍：真实持有者经 gRPC 拿到 manifest（能力 exp 落 60±5s），再用该段 URL 不带 Authorization 头取到与受保护 HTTP 逐字节一致的媒体；非持有者、数量不足（1<2／2<3）、双凭据冲突分别 PermissionDenied。真实故事：独立 cmd/microservices/api-gateway 入口漏把授权器传给 gRPC 服务，该模式下 gRPC 一律 Internal 而同一进程的 HTTP 正常——新增的第 9b 节以 4 个 FAIL 抓到（d2f1d81），此前只跑 monolith 的验收看不出来。

2. 链状态、缓存与撤销（8min）

  • 同区块证据：resolveAnchor 按配置 tag（默认 safe）一次解析 (number,hash)；verifier 的 block 参数必填（接口上无法悄悄 latest）；ERC-165 探测与余额同块；读后复核 header，期间换块即拒且不写缓存；无 prover / 解析失败 fail closed（用例断言零链上读）。
  • 缓存语义：存余额证据不存裁决——键 chain:wallet:contract:token:standard 不含 min_balance，每请求按当前规则重新比较；正证据总 age ≤5s 从原始 observedAt 起算，写缓存不刷新时间；负缓存 ≤2s；过期直接拒绝，无授权结果的过期容错。
  • reorg：检测回调原先在生产里从未注册，现接到 InvalidateNFTEvidence——只清该链证据；真实重组用 evm_snapshot/revert 构造并由生产检测器判定（非 sleep）。
  • 排障故事：记录型 RPC 代理抓到固定块实现残留的一次未固定 latest eth_call（detectTokenStandardCached 无条件先做未固定探测）——mock 单测看不见，真节点+代理才看得见。另一发现：Anvil 默认把 safe/finalized 钉在创世块（需 --slots-in-an-epoch 1），所以测试只断言"已进入 safe 视图的状态"。
  • 能力与撤销：60s TTL 是策略值（WithPlaybackTokenTTL 在 MaxPlaybackTokenTTL 钳制，零 TTL 回落策略值而非 30 分钟）；能力携带 sessionID + authEpoch + ruleVersion；登出推高 epoch → 已发能力 5s 内失效；规则版本与写入同一条 CTE 提交（迁移 036），版本变了旧能力被拒且规则还原后也不复活（还原是新版本）；嵌套 playlist 是续期点（≤5s 证据重查持仓），segment 只验能力不查链。
  • 70s：MaxTransferRevocationBound = 70s = 60+5+5 之和，ValidateProduction 校验总和（三项各自合规加起来仍可能超界）。历史缺陷：容差硬编码 30s ⇒ 真实窗口 90s，修复后实测过期落 67s。
  • 依赖故障：RPC 全断拒"新申请/续期"（503），已发能力在会话/规则/epoch 可验时可在原有效期内读 segment，不因链故障延长；Redis 断则受保护资源全拒而 /health 正常——拒绝受保护资源 ≠ 进程已死。

3. 真实媒体交付与实测排障（7min）

  • 验收先修对：旧脚本自签 HS256 JWT（从未走认证）、1KB 全零字节冒充视频。现在 token 全来自 challenge→sign→login；fixture 真媒体（640x360、h264+aac、10s、sha256 记录）并强制整段解码；缺工具/缺媒体 = FAIL 不 skip。
  • 原件可信：完成上传时服务端流式算 SHA-256，封存到 originals/<contentID>/<sha256><ext>（digest 在 key 里 ⇒ 不同字节不同 key）；再封存不同 digest → 409；从不采信客户端 hash；线上实证 DB file_hash=bf582157… 与封存对象 S3 GET 一致；verified digest 才进链上登记意图。
  • 输入安全：SSRF 精确白名单、解析主机后逐地址判定（公网+内网记录整体拒、解析失败拒）；断言是目标服务真实收到 0 请求（只判 4xx 不够——"先校验后照样 fetch"仍会过）；metadata IP / localhost / 外网 / file:// 线上全 400。
  • 媒体门：ffprobe 白名单（mp4+H.264+AAC）、600s/1080p 上界、独立 60s 超时 + 1MiB 输出上限；超限拒绝而非截断——原 LimitReader(2GiB) 到限后 io.Copy 返回 nil，会"成功"转码一个被截断的原件。
  • 任务权威：Postgres 是 task status 唯一来源；任务行与 outbox 意图同事务、PubAck 后标送达；跨实例 claim = 条件 UPDATE + lease（DB NOW() 计时）+ generation，失去 lease 无法提交；重试 = 同消息延迟 Nak、业务上限 3 次（含首次）；第 3 次失败 → failed + DLQ 意图同事务，发布成功才 Ack；GetStatus 不读进程 map。
  • generation 发布：输出进私有 g<N>/，全部校验通过后在 lease 守卫的事务里把 published_generation 单调向前切换——这次 UPDATE 就是发布本身；飞行中的 generation 拿着合法 capability 也读不到；失败只清自己的 g<N>/。
  • 信任域隔离：不可信媒体解析在 sg-fc-transcoder——运行态实测非 root、CapDrop=ALL、只读根 + tmpfs /tmp、1GiB/2cpu、env 无 JWT/私钥/RPC；执行归因 = DB lease_owner 非空 且 docker logs sg-fc-transcoder 命中 taskID（内嵌 worker 已禁用，无别的执行者）；容器缺失 = FAIL。
  • 真实 playlist：只重写 URI；EXT-X-KEY（加密）与未知带 URI 标签拒绝而非泄露对象路径；EXTINF 总和 ≈ 真实时长（10s fixture 实测 10.0s）、TARGETDURATION ≥ 最大 EXTINF（RFC 8216）；手写"每段 6 秒"只剩遗留回退。

4. 证据边界：压测与发布（4min）

  • 压测（✅ 已实测，口径先讲）：真实 HTTP client 把响应体读完并校验大小/hash，统计的是已授权 playlist + segment + init/key 的全部响应字节；固定 EXTINF 节奏、固定 rendition、每观众独立真实登录持 NFT；预热 10s / 采样 30s。实测（commit f4d65bc，fixture hash 086df28b…；后续权威集合见下）：1 观众 28/28、10 观众 280/280、50 观众 1400/1400 成功、0 错误、0 个 4xx，ttfb_p95 = 58.4 / 85.5 / 61.7ms —— 50 观众 ≥99% 门槛达标（实测 100%）。跑分还抓出一个真实合桶缺陷：IP 键控的全局限流器在 auth 之前把所有观众并进一个 manifest 桶（10 观众 120×429），媒体路径豁免后归零（f4d65bc，用例 TestRateLimitMiddleware_MediaRoutesAreNotIPMerged）。故障矩阵与 p95 回归比较随后已补齐并实测（00aff4e/346ac46）：cold_cache（重启 monolith 清 L1 + 只删 nft_cache:*，保留 session）、rpc_primary_down（monolith 配两个同链端点，停可停的 socat 主端点）、rpc_all_down（两个端点全停）——50 观众走回退仍 1400/1400；全停时 0 放行、0 媒体字节（判据落在 harness 内，单次放行即判失败）。同 runner 固定 reference 的 p95 比较：早期布线对齐后 ratio = 1.000（候选 55.98ms vs reference f20fb92 55.97ms，各 3 轮中位数，超 1.20× 即失败）；权威合并运行（候选 a2ed3b11，计划 §8.16 H）ratio = 1.029 ≤ 1.20（72.95 vs 70.89ms），引用以 1.029 为准。过程本身抓出两个真问题：候选被留在故障矩阵的两端点布线上而 reference 只能单端点，首测因此假报 ratio=1.456（改为同布线后 1.000）；reference 轮的 provenance 一度被标成候选 SHA（改用 PERF_COMMIT 标注）。1/10/50 × 3 重复与 100 观众容量探索已实测（全部 100% 成功、2800/2800，§8.16 H）；该矩阵取自本机固定预算运行，CI 的 perf job 只跑 harness / 指标开销门（非 live 矩阵）。仍未采：CPU/RSS。点名：test/performance/performance_test.go:TestConcurrentRequests 是 sleep 调度模拟，其数字不可引用。
  • 历史发布验收记录（不代表当前候选）：设计是一 SHA 一候选、扫描/部署/发布全程同 digest（image@sha256:）、临时栈空卷 + 真 migration + 真播放 smoke、恢复演练必须"B 确实失败 + A 恢复"双断言（rollback 成功不能洗绿 deploy 失败）；旧 schema 有 additive 迁移 + .down.sql 记录。历史结果：旧 verify-docker release stage 的空卷部署 smoke ALL 66 PASSED + 坏变体被拒 + 健康镜像恢复；部署命令失败的反向证明 EXIT=1（0 残留容器）；旧候选 ab172c2 的 run 39005594931 D 节点实跑空卷部署、migration、回滚兼容腿和三类失败演练（计划 §8.16 K.2）。当前候选 d0f05152 的 release 与同 SHA CI 已通过，完整本轮证据见 §8.86。生产 rollout 未做（无 target / secrets / 授权）。
  • 已落地的 CI 门一句话带过：覆盖率缺失/空文件即失败（自证 15/15，含 59.9% 必须失败）、迁移 ON_ERROR_STOP、Anvil 进候选门禁、smoke 改为"依赖就绪 → healthy → /health 200"（febdf7f）。

5. 收尾（2min；待本人按当前证据更新后再讲）：当前工程候选 d0f05152 的完整 all、同 SHA CI 和真实 demo 已通过；旧候选数字仍按历史来源解读。生产与本人事项不代填。

四、追问答案（一句话 → 关键点 → 边界）

Q1 nonce 原子性怎么保证？ 一句话：登录 challenge 与链上交易 nonce 是两套原子性——前者 Redis Lua 单命令消费，后者 Postgres advisory lock + 唯一约束。 关键点：链上 nonce 按 (chainID,signer) 取 max(DB+1, 链 pending)、UNIQUE(chain,signer,nonce) 兜底；先持久化再广播，超时是 unknown，恢复查 receipt 或重播同一 raw 字节绝不重签；最硬的故事是三个进程内分配器在真实栈互顶交易（验收 FAIL: Mint receipt status=missing）才收敛成 NonceLedger（0756ea4）。 测试：TestGenerateWalletChallenge_NonceIs128BitAndUnique、TestWalletAuth_ChallengeReuse_Rejected、TestAcceptance_NonceAcrossInstances、TestAcceptance_SharedNonceAuthority、TestAcceptance_BroadcastUnknownRecovery。 边界：这是平台代签侧的 nonce；用户自己钱包的 nonce 不归我管。

Q2 EIP-191 / SIWE 怎么验？ 一句话：EIP-191 是"对服务端下发的原始消息恢复地址再比对"；SIWE(EIP-4361) 把消息结构化，逐字段校验 domain/URI/chain/nonce/address。 关键点：地址比对用 subtle.ConstantTimeCompare；challenge 绑定地址+链+过期+已用，验签通过后原子消费才发 JWT；未知 signType 显式 ErrNotSupported（旧实现静默落到 personal_sign）；EIP-712、EIP-1271 另有回归。 测试：TestGenerateWalletChallenge_RejectsUnknownSigningType、TestAuthenticateWithWallet_ChallengeAlreadyUsed、TestEIP1271_IsValidSignature_Valid/Invalid、TestWalletAuth_ExpiredChallenge_Rejected。 边界：验证的是"这个地址签过这条消息"，不等于现实身份；nonce TTL 300s 就是登录窗口。

Q3 ERC-721/1155 数量语义？ 一句话：唯一授权信号 GetNFTOwnershipQuantity——集合 balanceOf、指定 ERC-721 是 0/1、指定 ERC-1155 读链上 uint256，按 quantity >= min_balance 比较。 关键点：ERC-165 是标准识别唯一真源（删掉 balanceOf 探测，它会把 ERC-20 误判成 NFT）；ERC-1155 collection 规则必须有 tokenID，"全 collection 总数"是虚构语义；缓存存证据不存裁决、min_balance 不进缓存键，低→高门槛不交叉放行；不支持固定块的客户端返回 Unknown 而非 latest。 测试：TestNFTGate_ERC1155Quantity_OneRejected_TwoAllowed、TestAnvil_ERC1155_Web3Service_NFTGate（mint 1→min2 拒、mint 2→放行）、TestNFTGate_CacheOrder*、修复前红后绿的 verifier_extended_test 旧用例。 边界：ERC-165 自报 true 只说明接口存在，不证明合约可信——规则发布者承担所选合约的信任边界。

Q4 为什么 safe/finalized？重组怎么办？ 一句话：latest 重组时会说谎；授权固定到 tag 解析出的一个 (number,hash)，读前后核对，重组即失效证据。 关键点：verifier block 参数必填（接口上无法回落）；ERC-165 与余额同块；证据 age ≤5s 起于原始 observedAt；重组事件只清该链；真实重组用 snapshot/revert 由生产检测器判定；代理抓到残留 latest eth_call 是排障亮点；Anvil safe 默认钉创世块（--slots-in-an-epoch 1 才推进）。 测试：TestResolveQuantity_NeverAnchorsAtLatest、…_BlockChangedDuringReadIsRejected、TestAnvil_BlockEvidenceIsPinnedAndChainScoped、TestAnvil_SnapshotRevertProducesARealReorg。 边界：safe 换来确认延迟（实测滞后约 32 块）；多 RPC 回退是 endpoint 级可用性，不是轻客户端密码学证明——provider 是信任前提。

Q5 播放 capability 为什么可信？ 一句话：NFT 校验通过后签发的短时 bearer 能力，携带钱包、内容、用途、audience、会话、规则版本，TTL 60s。 关键点：HLS.js 无法在分片上带 Authorization 故走 query——但存在从不直接授予，中间件密码学校验；playlist 是续期点（≤5s 重查），segment/init/key 只验能力+撤销版本（不逐段查链）；与 access token 同密钥同 issuer，靠 audience 区分互不冒用；playlist private, no-store、Referrer-Policy: no-referrer、错误不回显 token；与 JWT 同行时钱包必须一致（401）。 测试：TestJWTAuth_RejectsPlaybackCapabilityAsAccessToken、验收 A capability replayed as a Bearer token on a management API is refused (401)、…paired with another wallet's JWT is refused (401)、Manifest is Cache-Control: private, no-store。 边界：bearer 被复制后撤销/到期前可能被别人用；钱包绑定 ≠ 持有私钥证明；已下载内容不可收回。

Q6 70 秒撤销边界怎么来的？ 一句话：不是拍的常数——60s 能力 + 5s 正证据 + 5s 容差之和，配置校验连总和一起查。 关键点：起点是健康可信 RPC 的 safe 视图已包含转移；safe 没越块时仍可能允许访问。当前 d0f05152 两条部署腿的 HTTP/gRPC 转移到拒绝实测在 59.732–62.403 秒范围内。历史 67 秒观测为自然过期，不证明转移撤销；规则/会话撤销另有 ≤5 秒判据。 测试：TestCapabilityPolicy_TransferBoundIsTheSumOfItsParts、TestValidateProduction_RejectsASumOverTheBound；当前工件 sg-closeout-d0f05152/preserve/acceptance-raw-d0f05152/logs/media.log 的 M3_HTTP_TRANSFER_70S_PASS / M3_GRPC_TRANSFER_70S_PASS 是转移计时证据。历史 expired capability 断言单独作为到期行为记录。 边界：70s 是服务器授权窗口，不保证链 70s 内确认；RPC 观测延迟单列；对已下载内容无效。

Q7 播放器分片 / key 请求怎么走？ 一句话：链 [PlaybackTokenAuth, JWT, NFTGate]——playlist 重查链，分片只验 capability + 撤销版本，Range 用 206。 关键点：capability 在 query（HLS.js 限制）；EXT-X-MAP init 段重写到受保护路由；EXT-X-KEY 带 URI 拒绝不改写（不泄露对象路径）——AES-128 密钥门控是条件任务 SG-16 未实现；Range 单区间 206 + Content-Range，不可满足 416，多区间拒绝；播放器把 401/403 判为"授权结束"（清 src/销毁 hls/清 capability）而非网络重试，浏览器实测 401=true 403=true 500=false。 测试：byte_range_test、验收 Range request returns 206、Path traversal in the segment name refused、Playback token refused for another content。 边界：分片是明文 HLS——token-gated 访问控制，不是 DRM。

Q8 真实压测测哪些响应字节？（历史口径 + 数字；不是当前候选验收） 一句话：已授权 playlist、segment、init/key 的全部响应字节，每个响应体读完并校验大小/hash，失败不计入成功吞吐。 关键点：固定 EXTINF 节奏 + 固定 rendition + 每观众独立真实登录持 NFT（每观众一个真实钱包，节流登录 + dev-mint）；预热 10s / 采样 30s；TTFB、p50/95/99、错误分布、HTTP 状态分布；未授权放行 0 硬门。实测：1/10/50 观众 = 28/28、280/280、1400/1400 全成功 0 错误，字节 42070/420700/2103500，ttfb_p95 58.4/85.5/61.7ms（commit f4d65bc）。50 观众 ≥99% 达标（100%）。 必须提的排障故事：多钱包化之后 10 观众仍 120×429，定位到 IP 键控全局限流器在 auth 之前合桶（Prometheus http_requests_total 无 429 而 rate_limit_total{redis,denied}=290、容器日志 0 条 429 三重证据），媒体路径豁免后归零。 故障矩阵（cold_cache / rpc_primary_down / rpc_all_down）已实跑：50 观众走同链回退仍 1400/1400 无损，全端点停时 0 放行 / 0 媒体字节。reference/candidate 的 p95 回归比较已实现并实测：早期布线对齐后 ratio = 1.000（候选 55.98ms vs reference 55.97ms），权威合并运行（候选 a2ed3b11，计划 §8.16 H）ratio = 1.029 ≤ 1.20（72.95 vs 70.89ms，各 3 轮中位数，超 1.20× 即失败）——引用以 1.029 为准。1/10/50 × 3 重复与 100 观众容量探索已实测（全部 100% 成功，§8.16 H）；未采的如实说：CPU/RSS。 边界：test/performance/performance_test.go:TestConcurrentRequests 是 sleep 模拟，引用它数字的回答是错的。

Q9 发布 digest 与 schema 回滚？（下列为历史案例；当前候选证据见 §8.86） 一句话：设计是"一 SHA 一候选、全程同 digest、additive 迁移可回滚"；本地 release stage 已实跑（空卷部署 smoke ALL 66 PASSED + 坏变体被拒 + 健康镜像恢复），远端 ab172c2 同 SHA run 39005594931 的 D 节点也已实跑（临时空卷部署 + 真 migration + 回滚兼容腿 + 三类失败演练，计划 §8.16 K.2）。 关键点：候选清单 {candidateSHA, images:{service:digest}}，扫描/部署/发布只认 image@sha256:，提升不换 digest；临时栈空卷 + 真 migration + 真播放 smoke；恢复演练必须"B 确实失败 + A 恢复"双断言，rollback 成功不能掩盖 deploy 失败；schema 全 additive 且 .down.sql 齐全（036–040），迁移失败会红（ON_ERROR_STOP，删掉了 || true）。 测试：scripts/check-coverage_test.sh 15/15（含 59.9% 必须失败）、make test-ci COVERAGE_MIN=99 必须非零、smoke"就绪→healthy→/health 200"已在真实依赖栈实测；反向证明"部署命令失败 harness 必须非零退出"实测 EXIT=1。 边界：宿主构建是预检，本机 Docker 功能证据与同 SHA CI 分列——历史冻结候选 ab172c2 的同 SHA 远端 CI 已全绿（run 39005594931，55/55、零 skip），M5 的远端部分据此成立；生产 rollout 未做。

Q10 用了 AI 吗？你本人做了什么？ 一句话：用了，大部分代码 AI 生成；我的工作是设计决策、验收判据、评审与排障——AI 的代码生成不能自动转成"我独立设计实现"。 关键点：拿"实测才暴露"的缺陷当证据——Range 被忽略、规则缓存 30s、容差 30s 致 90s 窗口、固定块残留 latest eth_call、三 nonce 分配器互顶：这些都是判据先立住、实现被自己的判据打红；TRANSITION_PLAN.md §8 逐条记录缺陷与修复 SHA；分工清单见第六节【待本人确认】。 边界：核心链路（验签→门控→投递→转码）我能讲到第三层；外围模块指出哪些没深入；欢迎现场看代码。

Q11 哪里去中心化了？ 一句话：所有权判定在链上（任何人可自行复核），基础设施有意自托管——不把它说成完全去中心化。 关键点：这是"链上 NFT 权限 + 平台媒体服务"；RPC provider 是信任前提；规则表、存储、转码、管理是中心化服务（内容运营本来就是中心化行为）；链上 registry 是平台登记不是版权证明。 边界：不说"无需信任""生产高可用已验证""版权确权"；NFT 持有 ≠ 版权；bearer 与已下载内容的限制照实说。

Q12 同一份权限为什么 HTTP 和 gRPC 都要验？ 一句话：授权语义只允许有一份实现，两个入口是同一份授权器的两个传输层。 关键点：两入口共用授权器与规则快照（规则＋版本＋权威读取时间），能力只由带封印的结果签发；gRPC 按真实链上数量判阈值（不是布尔持仓），TTL 用共享 60 秒策略；同一请求带双凭据时钱包/会话/epoch 必须一致。验收第 9b 节在两条部署腿各验一遍——持有者经 gRPC 拿 manifest 并用不带 Authorization 头的 URL 取到与 HTTP 逐字节一致的媒体，非持有者／数量不足（1<2、2<3）／双凭据冲突全部 PermissionDenied；该节还抓到独立 api-gateway 漏传授权器导致 gRPC 全 Internal 的真实缺陷（d2f1d81）。 边界：两个入口共享的是授权语义，不是传输；gRPC 仍只在受信网络暴露，不因"内网"接受客户端伪造 actor。

五、两道现场小练习（本人操作 + 讲解，Docker 验法）

由本人现场定位、修改、验证、讲解；AI 不生成录屏、不代答。变量沿用第二节。

题一：min_balance 改为 2（阈值越过持有量，持有人余额 = 1）
# 0) 定位比较逻辑（讲解点：证据与裁决分离）
grep -n "MinBalance\|min" pkg/middleware/nft_gate.go            # evaluateGatingRules: quantity >= min
# 1) 基线 200
curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN_A" \
  $BASE/api/v1/streaming/$CONTENT_ID/manifest.m3u8              # → 200
# 2) 走 owner API 改（必须走 API：版本 bump 与规则写入是同一条语句/CTE）
curl -s -X PUT $BASE/api/v1/gating-rules/$RULE_ID \
  -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN_A" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{"min_balance":2}'   # → 200
# 3) DB 核对：min_balance=2 且 content_rule_versions.version +1
docker exec sg-fc-postgres psql -U postgres -d streamgate -c \
  "SELECT min_balance FROM content_gating_rules WHERE id='$RULE_ID'; SELECT * FROM content_rule_versions;"
# 4) ≤5s 界内轮询复验 → 403（ruleVersion 变化强制缓存重读）
until [ "$(curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN_A" \
      $BASE/api/v1/streaming/$CONTENT_ID/manifest.m3u8)" = 403 ]; do sleep 1; done
# 5) 改回 1 → 恢复 200

判分：单测 TestNFTGate_MinGreaterThanBalance_Denies（改后拒）/ TestNFTGate_MinEqualsBalance_Allows（改回放行）/ TestNFTGate_CacheOrderLowThenHigh_Denies（低→高不继承放行）；验收断言 Raising the rule threshold denies the holder on re-request (403)、Holder is served again once the threshold is restored；DB 里 min_balance=2 且版本 +1。 必须讲的三点：① 为什么走 API 不走裸 SQL——版本与规则同一条语句提交，裸 SQL 不 bump 版本，gate 读到版本没变就复用缓存，只能等本地 TTL 兜底（权威在服务端写路径）；② 缓存键故意不含 min_balance——存的是余额证据不是裁决，阈值改动对同一份证据立即生效（TestNFTGate_WarmAndColdAgree 断言缓存里是真实余额）；③ 归因纪律——必须得到 403，503 是"依赖不可核验"不能算阈值生效（我在验收里踩过：规则指向会 revert 的合约时，无关的 503 曾让断言假 PASS，现在 503 对该断言是显式 FAIL）。

题二：转移 NFT 后"续期失败"（重取 manifest 必须被拒）
# 0) 基线 200；token id 从链上枚举持有人实际持有的（不假设 id 范围）
curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN_A" \
  $BASE/api/v1/streaming/$CONTENT_ID/manifest.m3u8              # → 200
# 1) 转走并确认余额归零
cast send $DEMO_NFT "transferFrom(address,address,uint256)" $WALLET_A $WALLET_B $TOKEN_ID \
  --rpc-url $ANVIL --private-key $KEY_A
cast call $DEMO_NFT "balanceOf(address)(uint256)" $WALLET_A --rpc-url $ANVIL     # → 0
# 2) 推进 safe 并确认越过转移块（Anvil 实测滞后约 32 块；无流量 safe 不前进）
cast rpc anvil_mine 48 --rpc-url $ANVIL
cast rpc eth_getBlockByNumber safe false | python3 -c 'import sys,json;print(int(json.load(sys.stdin)["number"],16))'
# 3) 续期（重取 manifest 即续期点，≤5s 证据重查）→ 403
curl -s -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' -H "Authorization: Bearer $TOKEN_A" \
  $BASE/api/v1/streaming/$CONTENT_ID/manifest.m3u8              # → 403
# 4) 转回必须用 B（当前持有人）私钥；用 A 的私钥会 revert；再挖块
cast send $DEMO_NFT "transferFrom(address,address,uint256)" $WALLET_B $WALLET_A $TOKEN_ID \
  --rpc-url $ANVIL --private-key $KEY_B
cast rpc anvil_mine 48 --rpc-url $ANVIL                          # 复验 → 200

判分：验收断言 Transferred the holder's N token(s) away (balance now 0)、The safe view now includes the transfer、Transferring the holding away denies the next manifest (403)、Transferring the holding back restores access (200)；单测 TestCachedQuantity_ReorgStillDetectedWithinChain、TestAnvil_SnapshotRevertProducesARealReorg；"旧能力还能播多久"答 Q6（safe 视图确认后 70s 上界；转移计时使用 M3 HTTP/gRPC 标记）。 必须讲的三点：① 顺序——先断言余额归零、再断言 safe 越块、最后判 403，顺序错了会把一次失败的转移误读成"gate 没生效"（safe 没越块时 200 是正确行为）；② 转移后续期失败 ≠ 每个分片查链——manifest/playlist 是续期点，segment 只验能力与撤销版本；③ 转回必须用当前持有人私钥签名，否则 revert 静默失败，会把"非持有人被拒"变成假 PASS。

六、个人贡献与 AI 归属映射（M6 人工确认清单）

诚实原则（计划原文）：AI 的代码生成不能自动转成"我独立设计实现"。投递前由本人逐条确认。

主张 性质 证据 / 说明 状态

七、信任边界话术（被问到就照说）

  1. "这是链上 NFT 权限 + 平台媒体服务：平台控制规则、播放能力、存储、转码与管理；RPC provider 提供链状态——多 RPC 回退不等于轻客户端的密码学证明。"
  2. "NFT 持有是观看资格，不等于媒体版权；链上 registry 是平台登记（attestation），不是版权确权。"
  3. "不说完全去中心化、不说无需信任、不说生产高可用已验证——这三样我都没有证据。"
  4. "capability 是 bearer：被复制后撤销/到期前可能被别人用；钱包绑定不等于持有私钥的证明；跨钱包负例覆盖 JWT 主体冲突与签名篡改，不是防分享。"
  5. "已下载的媒体、播放器缓冲、已拿到的密钥不可远程收回——所以它是 token-gated 访问控制，不是 DRM，我也不这么包装它。"
  6. "旧候选 ab172c2 的 run 39005594931 为历史记录。历史代码候选 4eae43d6 的本机完整 Docker 门已通过，run 40608718226 的同 SHA CI attempt 10 已失败。当前 d0f05152 已有完整 all、同 SHA CI 55/55 和 demo 两腿各 77/77 的独立证据；旧记录不用于代替当前证据。生产 rollout 未做。"

Service Level Objectives

This document defines the formal SLOs, error budget policy, and burn-rate alerting for the StreamGate platform. It follows the Google SRE Workbook methodology.

SLO Table

Service Indicator Target Window Measurement
API Gateway Request Availability 99.9% 30d rolling (1 - 5xx / total) * 100
API Gateway P99 Latency < 2000ms 5m sliding histogram_quantile(0.99)
API Gateway P95 Latency < 500ms 5m sliding histogram_quantile(0.95)
NFT Verification Request Success 99.5% 30d rolling (1 - error / total) * 100
RPC Providers Aggregate Availability 99.5% 30d rolling failover rate monitoring
Cache Hit Rate NFT Access Cache > 85% 5m sliding cache hit / total queries

Error Budgets

SLO Monthly Budget Budget Details
API 99.9% 0.1% of requests e.g., 43min downtime/month at 1000 rpm
NFT 99.5% 0.5% of requests e.g., 3.6h of degraded service/month
Budget consumption triggers
Burn Rate Window Action Example
>= 10x 1h Page oncall (critical) Budget exhausted in ~3 days
>= 3x 6h Ticket (warning) Budget exhausted in ~10 days
>= 1x 24h Monitor (notice) On-track to exhaust in 30d
Deployment freeze

When error budget is exhausted:

Condition: slo:api:error_budget_remaining < 0
Action:    All production deploys frozen
Exception: Hotfixes for the issue consuming the budget
Reset:     Budget resets at the start of each calendar month

Prometheus Rules

  • config/alerts/slo_recording_rules.yml — precomputed SLO metrics
  • config/alerts/slo_burnrate_alerts.yml — multi-window burn-rate alerts

Monthly SLO Review

At the end of each month, the oncall team should:

  1. Check SLO compliance for the past 30 days:
    slo:api:compliance_30d
  2. Review the top 3 sources of error budget consumption (by route, by status code)
  3. Decide if SLO targets need adjustment for the next month
  4. Document the review in the team's monthly post-mortem

Excluded from SLO

The following are intentionally excluded from availability calculations:

  • /health and /metrics endpoints (internal monitoring — can return 503 during dependency outages without affecting user-facing availability)
  • Rate-limited requests (429 responses are client errors, not service failures)
  • Requests to undefined routes (404s from path scanning / bots)
  • Pre-production environments (dev, test, staging)

Related Documents

  • config/alerts/streamgate_alerts.yml — existing threshold-based alerts
  • docs/operations/operational-excellence.md — incident severity definitions
  • test/README.md — performance targets as tested in CI

8.86 2026-11-02 最终独立验收与交付冻结

结论：PASS WITH BOUNDARIES。 SG-00～SG-15 的工程与 M6 AI 成品范围完成。代码候选为 d0f05152edc1fb404cb2d78b734c95244e1a7a4e，CI 为 run 41343759379 / attempt 3。本节是后续证据记录，文档提交不是新的代码候选、不继承或冒充它自己的 CI 绿灯；已验收的 C 不再修改，不因回填本节制造验证循环。

Returns the previous used_at value139// (empty string if unused, non-empty if already used). This prevents the140// TOCTOU race where two concurrent requests both read a challenge as unused141// and both receive valid JWTs.142var markChallengeUsedLua = redis.NewScript(`143local data = redis.call('GET', KEYS[1])144if not data then145  return 'NOT_FOUND'146end147local decoded = cjson.decode(data)148if decoded.used_at and decoded.used_at ~= '' and decoded.used_at ~= '0001-01-01T00:00:00Z' then149  return 'ALREADY_USED'150end151decoded.used_at = ARGV[1]152local encoded = cjson.encode(decoded)153redis.call('SET', KEYS[1], encoded, 'PX', ARGV[2])154return 'OK'155`)156157// MarkChallengeUsed marks a challenge used in Redis atomically via Lua script.158func (r *RedisChallengeStore) MarkChallengeUsed(ctx context.Context, id string, usedAt time.Time) error {159	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, 3*time.Second)160	defer cancel()161162	// Calculate remaining TTL in milliseconds163	ttlMs := int64(r.ttl / time.Millisecond)164165	result, err := markChallengeUsedLua.Run(ctx, r.client, []string{id},166		usedAt.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339Nano), ttlMs).Result()167	if err != nil {168		return fmt.Errorf("failed to mark challenge used: %w", err)169	}170171	str, ok := result.(string)172	if !ok {173		return fmt.Errorf("unexpected Lua script result type: %T", result)174	}175176	switch str {177	case "OK":178		return nil179	case "ALREADY_USED":180		return ErrChallengeUsed181	case "NOT_FOUND":182		return ErrChallengeNotFound183	}184	return nil185}186187// Close closes the Redis connection.188func (r *RedisChallengeStore) Close() error {189	if r.client != nil {190		return r.client.Close()191	}192	return nil193}

483func normalizedMinBalance(minBalance int) (int64, error) {484	switch {485	case minBalance == 0:486		return 1, nil487	case minBalance < 0:488		return 0, &invalidGatingRuleError{reason: "min_balance must not be negative"}489	default:490		return int64(minBalance), nil491	}492}493494// PolicyDecision is the outcome of evaluating a rule set against a wallet: which495// rule granted access, at which anchor. It is exported so the business layer can496// authorize playback with the SAME tested evaluation the HTTP gate uses.497type PolicyDecision struct {498	Allowed            bool499	GrantingRule       *GatingRule500	Anchor             *BlockHeaderInfo501	EvidenceObservedAt time.Time502}503504// EvaluatePolicy applies OR semantics over the active rules: any rule that505// verifies to a sufficient quantity grants access. If no rule grants access, a506// rule that could not be verified at all produces a service error so the caller507// answers 503 rather than a misleading 403.508//509// On success it also returns which rule granted and the block it was read at, so510// the caller can bind an authorization to a specific chain state.511func EvaluatePolicy(ctx context.Context, config *NFTGateConfig, logger *zap.Logger, rules []GatingRule, walletAddress string) (PolicyDecision, error) {512	var firstUnverifiable error513514	for _, rule := range rules {515		if err := validateGatingRule(rule); err != nil {516			return PolicyDecision{}, err517		}518		minBalance, _ := normalizedMinBalance(rule.MinBalance)519520		cacheKey := nftCacheKey(rule.ChainID, walletAddress, rule.ContractAddress, rule.TokenID, rule.Standard)521		quantity, anchor, observedAt, err := resolveQuantityObserved(ctx, config, logger, cacheKey, rule.ChainID, rule.ContractAddress, rule.TokenID, walletAddress)522		if err != nil {523			logger.Warn("gating rule could not be verified",524				zap.String("contract", rule.ContractAddress),525				zap.Int64("chain_id", rule.ChainID),526				zap.Error(err))527			if firstUnverifiable == nil {528				firstUnverifiable = err529			}530			continue531		}532533		if quantity.Cmp(big.NewInt(minBalance)) >= 0 {534			granting := rule535			return PolicyDecision{Allowed: true, GrantingRule: &granting, Anchor: anchor, EvidenceObservedAt: observedAt}, nil536		}537	}538539	if firstUnverifiable != nil {540		return PolicyDecision{}, firstUnverifiable541	}542	return PolicyDecision{Allowed: false}, nil543}544545// evaluateGatingRules keeps the boolean shape the HTML gate has always used,

594func isPlaylistRequest(c *gin.Context) bool {595	return strings.HasSuffix(c.FullPath(), playlistRouteSuffix)596}597598// resolveAnchor picks the ONE block an authorization reads at: the header at the599// configured finality tag, resolved once. Every decision query in the request600// then names that block number, so the node never gets to re-resolve a tag601// mid-read and hand back two different chain states.602func resolveAnchor(ctx context.Context, config *NFTGateConfig, chainID int64) (*BlockHeaderInfo, error) {603	if config.BlockProver == nil {604		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no block prover configured; refusing to authorize without block evidence")605	}606	tag := strings.TrimSpace(string(config.BlockTag))607	if tag == "" {608		// Fail-closed default: the safe tag, never "latest".609		tag = "safe"610	}611	header, err := config.BlockProver.HeaderByTag(ctx, chainID, tag)612	if err != nil {613		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to resolve %s block on chain %d: %w", tag, chainID, err)614	}615	if header == nil || header.Hash == "" {616		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolved %s block on chain %d carries no hash", tag, chainID)617	}618	return header, nil619}620621// verifyAnchorUnchanged re-reads the anchor after the decision queries and622// refuses if its hash moved. A block that changed under the read means the623// evidence was assembled from a chain state that no longer exists.624func verifyAnchorUnchanged(ctx context.Context, config *NFTGateConfig, chainID int64, anchor *BlockHeaderInfo) error {625	after, err := config.BlockProver.HeaderByNumber(ctx, chainID, big.NewInt(int64(anchor.Number)))626	if err != nil {627		return fmt.Errorf("failed to re-read block %d on chain %d: %w", anchor.Number, chainID, err)628	}629	if after == nil {630		return fmt.Errorf("block %d on chain %d disappeared during the read", anchor.Number, chainID)631	}632	if after.Hash != anchor.Hash {633		return fmt.Errorf("block %d on chain %d changed during the read (%s -> %s)",634			anchor.Number, chainID, anchor.Hash, after.Hash)635	}636	return nil637}638639// entryAnchor reconstructs the block a cached entry was read at. An entry with640// no recorded block carries no anchor to report.641func entryAnchor(entry NFTAccessEntry) *BlockHeaderInfo {642	if entry.BlockHash == "" {643		return nil644	}645	return &BlockHeaderInfo{Number: entry.BlockNumber, Hash: entry.BlockHash}646}647648// quantityEvidence pairs a quantity with the block it was read at, so the caller649// can report WHICH chain state authorized the request.650type quantityEvidence struct {

issuing one to an empty wallet would mint an420	// unattributable credential.421	if walletAddress == "" {422		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("generate_playback_token", "failure").Inc()423		return "", errors.New("wallet address is required for playback token")424	}425426	_, span := monitoring.StartOTelSpan(ctx, "auth.generate_playback_token",427		attribute.String("content_id", contentID),428		attribute.Int64("chain_id", chainID),429	)430	defer span.End()431432	if ttl <= 0 {433		// The policy default, not a second hardcoded window: a caller that434		// forgets the TTL must not get a 30-minute capability by accident.435		ttl = s.PlaybackTokenTTL()436	}437438	opts := playbackTokenOptions{}439	for _, apply := range options {440		apply(&opts)441	}442	if opts.sessionID == "" {443		// A capability must inherit the caller's login session. Creating a444		// separate session here would leave media already handed to a player445		// valid past the caller's logout — the exact thing session binding446		// exists to prevent. Transports must supply the requesting session.447		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("generate_playback_token", "failure").Inc()448		return "", errors.New("a capability must inherit the caller's session")449	}450	if !opts.ruleVersionSet {451		// The version the capability is issued under comes from the authority, so452		// a capability is never minted with a version that was guessed. 593func (s *AuthService) ParsePlaybackToken(ctx context.Context, tokenString, contentID, clientFingerprint string) (*Claims, error) {594	_, span := monitoring.StartOTelSpan(ctx, "auth.parse_playback_token",595		attribute.String("content_id", contentID),596	)597	defer span.End()598599	// Verified against the capability key ONLY: with key separation an access600	// token can never pass here even before the audience check, because the601	// two credential kinds are signed with independent keys.602	claims, err := s.parseTokenCapability(tokenString)603	if err != nil {604		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "failure").Inc()605		return nil, err606	}607	if claims.TokenType != CapabilityTokenType {608		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "failure").Inc()609		return nil, errors.New("not a playback token")610	}611	// The audience keeps a credential minted for one purpose from being spent on612	// another. Both are signed with the same key, so this is the discriminator.613	if !claimAudienceContains(claims.Audience, PlaybackCapabilityAudience) {614		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "failure").Inc()615		return nil, errors.New("playback token audience mismatch")616	}617	if claims.Subject != contentID {618		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "failure").Inc()619		return nil, errors.New("playback token content mismatch")620	}621	// A fingerprint-bound token must be presented with the same fingerprint —622	// presenting NO fingerprint does not satisfy the binding.623	if claims.ClientFingerprint != "" && claims.ClientFingerprint != clientFingerprint {624		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "failure").Inc()625		return nil, errors.New("playback token fingerprint mismatch")626	}627	if s.blacklist != nil && claims.ID != "" {628		if s.blacklist.IsRevoked(ctx, claims.ID) {629			monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "failure").Inc()630			return nil, ErrTokenRevoked631		}632	}633	// The revocation version. Every playlist, segment, init and key request634	// resolves the capability through here, so this is the one place that has to635	// hold for revocation to reach media already in flight. Without it, logging636	// out would only stop the exact token presented and a capability already637	// handed to a player would keep working for its whole TTL.638	//639	// Presence is required, not defaulted: a capability minted before sessions640	// existed has no epoch, and treating "absent" as "current" would let such a641	// credential bypass revocation entirely.642	if claims.SessionID == "" {643		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "failure").Inc()644		return nil, errors.New("playback token is not bound to a session")645	}646	if !s.SessionValid(claims.SessionID, claims.AuthEpoch) {647		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "failure").Inc()648		return nil, ErrTokenRevoked649	}650	// The recovery epoch. Every playlist, segment, init and key request goes651	// through here, so a restore that rotates the epoch stops media already in652	// flight everywhere at once — not just for the login session that requested it.653	if err := s.requireCurrentRestoreEpoch(claims.RestoreEpoch); err != nil {654		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "failure").Inc()655		return nil, err656	}657	// The authorization-rule version. A segment request cannot afford a chain658	// query, so the capability carries the version it was issued under and is659	// refused once the content's rules move. Without this, raising a rule's660	// threshold or retargeting it would not reach media already in flight until661	// the capability's TTL happened to expire.662	if current, err := s.currentRuleVersion(ctx, claims.Subject); err != nil {663		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "failure").Inc()664		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read the rule version: %w", err)665	} else if current != claims.RuleVersion {666		monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "failure").Inc()667		return nil, ErrTokenRevoked668	}669	monitoring.AuthOperationsTotal.WithLabelValues("parse_playback_token", "success").Inc()670	return claims, nil671}672673// ValidatePlaybackToken validates a playback token, ensures it matches the674// requested content, and enforces that it was issued to walletAddress. The675// wallet is REQUIRED — passing an empty wallet is an error, not a skip — which676// forces every call site to prove whose wallet it is checking against and677// prevents the silent no-wallet-compare downgrade this check used to have.

1package transcoding23func taskSnapshot(task *TranscodingTask) *TranscodingTask {4	snapshot := *task5	snapshot.Metadata = make(map[string]interface{}, len(task.Metadata))6	for key, value := range task.Metadata {7		snapshot.Metadata[key] = metadataSnapshot(value)8	}9	if task.StartedAt != nil {10		started := *task.StartedAt11		snapshot.StartedAt = &started12	}13	if task.CompletedAt != nil {14		completed := *task.CompletedAt15		snapshot.CompletedAt = &completed16	}17	return &snapshot18}1920func metadataSnapshot(value interface{}) interface{} {21	switch value := value.(type) {22	case map[string]interface{}:23		if value == nil {24			return nil25		}26		snapshot := make(map[string]interface{}, len(value))27		for key, entry := range value {28			snapshot[key] = metadataSnapshot(entry)29		}30		return snapshot31	case []interface{}:32		if value == nil {33			return nil34		}35		snapshot := make([]interface{}, len(value))36		for index, entry := range value {37			snapshot[index] = metadataSnapshot(entry)38		}39		return snapshot40	default:41		return value42	}43}

207// PublishOutbox publishes an outbox payload with a stable message ID. The208// message ID is set both as the JetStream dedupe key (nats.MsgId) and as an209// explicit header before the call so the dedupe key is visible end to end.210// Redelivery/duplicate publish is therefore idempotent at the broker; the DB211// state machine remains the real guarantee.212func (q *NATSTranscodingQueue) PublishOutbox(kind, msgID string, payload []byte) error {213	var subject string214	switch kind {215	case models.OutboxKindTask:216		subject = jsStreamSubject217	case models.OutboxKindDLQ:218		subject = jsDLQStreamSubject219	default:220		return fmt.Errorf("unknown outbox kind: %s", kind)221	}222223	msg := &nats.Msg{224		Subject: subject,225		Data:    payload,226		Header:  make(nats.Header),227	}228	if msgID != "" {229		msg.Header.Set(nats.MsgIdHdr, msgID)230	}231	if _, err := q.js.PublishMsg(msg, nats.MsgId(msgID)); err != nil {232		return fmt.Errorf("failed to publish %s message to JetStream: %w", kind, err)233	}234	return nil235}236237func (q *NATSTranscodingQueue) Enqueue(task *models.TranscodingTask) error {238	data, err := json.Marshal(task)239	if err != nil {240		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal task: %w", err)241	}242	return q.PublishOutbox(models.OutboxKindTask, task.ID, data)243}244

223func (p *Publisher) drivePending(ctx context.Context, in *Intent) error {224	digest, err := DecodeDigest(in.ContentDigest)225	if err != nil {226		return p.store.MarkFailed(ctx, in.ContentID, StatusPending, "invalid persisted digest: "+err.Error())227	}228229	chainPending, err := p.client.GetNonce(ctx, p.cfg.Signer)230	if err != nil {231		// Transient: leave pending for the next pass. The intent is durable.232		p.log.Warn("registry: chain nonce unavailable", zap.String("content_id", in.ContentID), zap.Error(err))233		return nil234	}235236	nonce, err := p.store.ReserveNonce(ctx, p.cfg.Signer, chainPending)237	if err != nil {238		return err239	}240241	signed, err := p.pipeline.PrepareRegisterContent(ctx, in.ContentID, in.Creator, digest, in.MetadataURI, nonce)242	if err != nil {243		// Nothing was broadcast, so the reserved nonce is provably unused and244		// can go back to the ledger (A.4's recycling rule).245		if rerr := p.store.ReleaseReservation(ctx, p.cfg.Signer, nonce); rerr != nil {246			p.log.Warn("registry: release reservation after prepare failure", zap.Error(rerr))247		}248		if in.Attempt+1 >= p.cfg.MaxAttempts {249			return p.store.MarkFailed(ctx, in.ContentID, StatusPending, "sign/register tx: "+err.Error())250		}251		return p.store.execGuarded(ctx, `attempt = attempt + 1, last_error = $5`, in.ContentID, StatusPending, truncateErr("sign/register tx: "+err.Error()))252	}253254	// A.4: persist the signed raw tx, nonce, hash and intent BEFORE broadcast.255	// If this write fails, the tx is NOT broadcast — an unwritable database256	// must never produce an untracked chain write. After this transaction the257	// intent row is already 'submitted', so the broadcast outcome transitions258	// are guarded on 'submitted', not on the pre-persist status.259	if err := p.persistBroadcastFacts(ctx, in, signed); err != nil {260		return err261	}262263	// The signed raw tx, its nonce and its hash survive in the DB, but nothing264	// has been handed to a node yet: a crash here must reuse exactly these bytes.265	p.arriveAt(ctx, "publisher.before_broadcast", in.ContentID)266267	return p.broadcast(ctx, in, signed, StatusSubmitted)268}269270// persistBroadcastFacts writes attempt + submitted intent in ONE transaction.271func (p *Publisher) persistBroadcastFacts(ctx context.Context, in *Intent, signed *tx.SignedTx) error {272	attempt := &Attempt{273		ReservationKey: ReservationKey(p.cfg.ChainID, p.cfg.Signer, signed.Nonce),274		ChainID:        p.cfg.ChainID,275		Signer:         p.cfg.Signer,276		Nonce:          int64(signed.Nonce),277		TxHash:         signed.Hash,278		RawTx:          hex.EncodeToString(signed.Raw),279		Status:         "signed",280	}281	err := p.store.DB().InTransaction(ctx, func(sqlTx *sql.Tx) error {282		if err := p.store.EnsureIntentExec(ctx, sqlTx, in.ContentID, in.Creator, in.ContentDigest, in.MetadataURI); err != nil {283			return err284		}285		if err := p.store.InsertAttemptExec(ctx, sqlTx, attempt); err != nil {286			return err287		}288		return p.store.MarkSubmittedExec(ctx, sqlTx, in.ContentID, signed.Hash, signed.Nonce, in.Status)289	})290	if err != nil {291		return fmt.Errorf("registry: persist broadcast facts for %s: %w (not broadcasting)", in.ContentID, err)292	}293	in.Status = StatusSubmitted294	in.TxHash = signed.Hash295	return nil296}297298// broadcast sends the persisted raw tx. Ambiguous failures (timeouts, conn299// resets) are broadcast_unknown: the nonce is NEVER rolled back and no new tx300// is signed (A.4). Definitive rejections are adjudicated against the chain301// nonce.302func (p *Publisher) broadcast(ctx context.Context, in *Intent, signed *tx.SignedTx, fromStatus string) error {303	err := p.pipeline.Broadcast(ctx, signed)304	if err == nil || isAlreadyKnown(err) {305		// The node has (or is known to have) the tx, but the caller's status306		// transition has not been written yet: a crash here must re-send these307		// exact persisted bytes, never re-sign (A.4).308		p.arriveAt(ctx, "publisher.after_send", in.ContentID)309		if fromStatus == StatusBroadcastUnknown {310			// A recovery re-send of the SAME raw tx succeeded (or the node311			// already had it): back to submitted.312			return p.store.MarkSubmittedExisting(ctx, in.ContentID, fromStatus)313		}314		return nil315	}316	if isAmbiguous(err) {317		p.log.Warn("registry: broadcast outcome unknown; keeping nonce, will re-send same raw tx",318			zap.String("content_id", in.ContentID), zap.String("tx_hash", signed.Hash), zap.Error(err))319		return p.store.MarkBroadcastUnknown(ctx, in.ContentID, fromStatus, "broadcast outcome unknown: "+err.Error())320	}321	return p.adjudicateRejection(ctx, in, signed, fromStatus, err)322}323324// recoverUnknown: broadcast_unknown → receipt lookup by the KNOWN hash first,325// then re-send the SAME raw tx (A.4). It never signs a new nonce.326func (p *Publisher) recoverUnknown(ctx context.Context, in *Intent) error {327	if in.TxHash == "" {328		return p.store.MarkFailed(ctx, in.ContentID, StatusBroadcastUnknown, "broadcast_unknown without a persisted tx hash")329	}330331	receipt, err := p.client.TransactionReceipt(ctx, common.HexToHash(in.TxHash))332	if err == nil && receipt != nil {333		return p.handleReceipt(ctx, in, receipt)334	}335336	attempt, aerr := p.store.GetAttempt(ctx, in.TxHash)337	if aerr != nil {338		return aerr339	}340	if attempt == nil {341		// Defensive: the attempt row is written before broadcast, so this only342		// happens if someone deleted history.343		return p.store.MarkFailed(ctx, in.ContentID, StatusBroadcastUnknown, "no persisted raw tx for "+in.TxHash)344	}345	raw, derr := hex.DecodeString(attempt.RawTx)346	if derr != nil {347		return p.store.MarkFailed(ctx, in.ContentID, StatusBroadcastUnknown, "persisted raw tx is not hex: "+derr.Error())348	}349	signed := &tx.SignedTx{Raw: raw, Hash: attempt.TxHash, Nonce: uint64(attempt.Nonce)}350	return p.broadcast(ctx, in, signed, StatusBroadcastUnknown)351}352353// recoverSubmitted: submitted with a persisted attempt but no receipt is the354// crash window between persistBroadcastFacts and the post-broadcast write —355// the process died before, during or right after handing the tx to a node.356// Receipt lookup by the KNOWN hash first, then re-send the SAME raw tx (A.4).357// It never signs a new nonce.
← 返回学习位置

1// Package registry implements SG-13 A (登记与交易): durable off-chain2// consistency for the on-chain content registration flow.3//4// The chain write is NEVER atomic with the DB write. Business success means5// the media and the register INTENT are committed; the chain registration is a6// separate, visible status tracked by the publisher loop. Every state the7// intent can be in is persisted in Postgres, so nothing lives only in a8// process map and nothing is "recovered" by re-reading a log line.9//10// Intent lifecycle (persisted in content_registry_intents):11//12//	pending          intent recorded in the same tx as the content row13//	submitted        signed raw tx persisted, then broadcast accepted (or the14//	                 node is known to have the tx)15//	confirmed        receipt.status=1 AND head >= receipt block + N confirmations16//	broadcast_unknown broadcast outcome ambiguous (RPC timeout): the nonce is17//	                 kept, the SAME raw tx is re-sent after a receipt lookup18//	failed           definitively dead (revert, or nonce consumed by another tx)19//	disabled         registry not configured (no intent row is created)20//21// Transition guards: every UPDATE is conditioned on the current status so two22// publishers racing on a lease boundary cannot stomp each other's outcome.23package registry2425import (26	"context"27	"database/sql"28	"encoding/hex"29	"fmt"30	"strings"31	"time"3233	"github.com/rtcdance/streamgate/pkg/storage"34	"github.com/rtcdance/streamgate/pkg/web3/tx"35)3637// Intent status vocabulary (SG-13 A.1). A log line is not a status.38const (39	StatusPending          = "pending"40	StatusSubmitted        = "submitted"41	StatusConfirmed        = "confirmed"42	StatusFailed           = "failed"43	StatusBroadcastUnknown = "broadcast_unknown"44	StatusDisabled         = "disabled"45)4647// Intent is one durable register intent: unique on (chain_id, registry_address,48// content_id). A client HTTP retry can therefore never create a second chain49// write for the same content.50type Intent struct {51	ContentID       string52	ChainID         int6453	RegistryAddress string54	Creator         string55	ContentDigest   string56	MetadataURI     string57	Status          string58	TxHash          string59	Nonce           sql.NullInt6460	BlockNumber     sql.NullInt6461	BlockHash       string62	ConfirmedAt     sql.NullTime63	LastError       string64	Attempt         int65	CreatedAt       time.Time66	UpdatedAt       time.Time67}6869// Attempt is one signed raw transaction, persisted BEFORE broadcast.70type Attempt struct {71	ReservationKey string72	ChainID        int6473	Signer         string74	Nonce          int6475	TxHash         string76	RawTx          string77	Status         string78}7980// ReservationKey links an attempt to its nonce reservation. The same key is81// reused for an explicit same-nonce replacement, which adds an attempt row but82// never a new reservation.83func ReservationKey(chainID int64, signer string, nonce uint64) string {84	return fmt.Sprintf("%d|%s|%d", chainID, strings.ToLower(signer), nonce)85}8687// Store is the Postgres-backed intent/nonce/attempt repository plus the88// registry coordinates (chain id + contract address) every write is scoped to.89type Store struct {90	db              storage.DB91	chainID         int6492	registryAddress string93	// ledger is the SHARED tx.NonceLedger: one allocator implementation, one94	// reservations table, for every signer path in the process.95	ledger      *tx.NonceLedger96	nonceClient tx.NonceClient97}9899// StoreOption customises Store construction.100type StoreOption func(*Store)101102// WithNonceClient injects the chain client the shared nonce ledger needs to103// prime a first allocation and to adjudicate releasing an attempt-referenced104// nonce. Callers that always pass their own chainPending hint (and never105// release signed nonces) do not need it.106func WithNonceClient(client tx.NonceClient) StoreOption {107	return func(s *Store) {108		s.nonceClient = client109	}110}111112func NewStore(db storage.DB, chainID int64, registryAddress string, opts ...StoreOption) *Store {113	s := &Store{114		db:              db,115		chainID:         chainID,116		registryAddress: strings.ToLower(registryAddress),117	}118	for _, opt := range opts {119		opt(s)120	}

1// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT2pragma solidity ^0.8.24;34import {Ownable} from "openzeppelin-contracts/contracts/access/Ownable.sol";5import {Ownable2Step} from "openzeppelin-contracts/contracts/access/Ownable2Step.sol";67/// Platform content attestation registry.8///9/// This contract records that the platform operator (the contract owner, i.e.10/// the `registrar`) attested to a content's existence and to the SHA-256 digest11/// of its original bytes. It is NOT a copyright registry and NOT an uploader12/// identity proof: `creator` is the verified platform-side owner of the13/// content, passed in by the operator, and the operator is always recoverable14/// from the transaction sender — the two roles are never conflated.15///16/// State machine (terminal states are final):17///18///     Nonexistent --register--> Active19///     Active  <--- owner --->  Flagged        (onlyOwner, both directions)20///     Active | Flagged -- owner or creator --> Removed   (terminal)21///22/// A contentId can never return to Nonexistent, a Removed InvalidPagination();78    error RenounceDisabled();7980    constructor() Ownable(msg.sender) {}8182    /// Ownership can only be handed over via the two-step transfer; renouncing83    /// would creator,117            contentDigest: contentDigest,118            metadataURI: metadataURI,119            status: ContentStatus.Active,120            createdAt: block.timestamp,121            updatedAt: block.timestamp122        });123124        _contentIds.push(contentId);125        _creatorContents[creator].push(contentId);126127        emit ContentRegistered(key, contentId, creator, contentDigest, metadataURI, block.timestamp);128    }129130    /// Only Active <-> Flagged transitions are reachable here, and only the131    /// platform owner may perform them. re.compile(r"<!--\s*streamgate-acceptance-status:\s*(incomplete|complete)\s*-->")545556def sha256(path):57    digest = hashlib.sha256()58    with path.open("rb") as stream:59        for chunk in manifest_path, ci_run, ci_attempt, event, registry_dir, registry_repository):38    source = manifest.get("registryPublication", {})39    if event == "pull_request":40        if source != {"status": "NOT_RECORDED"} or registry_dir is not None or registry_repository is not None:41            raise ValueError("PRs must not claim a registry publication")42        return source, None43    expected = {"status": "RECORDED", "ciRun": ci_run, "ciAttempt": ci_attempt}44    if source == expected and registry_dir is None and registry_repository is None:45        return source, None46    attempt = package signature

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"math/big"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum"
	"github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/common"
	"github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/core/types"
	"github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/crypto"
	"github.com/ethereum/go-ethereum/rpc"
	"go.uber.org/zap"
)

const (
	AccountEOA                = "eoa"
	AccountContract           = "erc1271"
	MaxWalletSignatureBytes   = 8192
	WalletVerificationTimeout = 5 * time.Second
	MaxWalletProofAge         = 5 * time.Second
	WalletCallGasLimit        = 500000
)

var (
	ErrWalletFormat      = errors.New("malformed wallet proof")
	ErrWalletUnsupported = errors.New("unsupported wallet or signing scheme")
	ErrWalletDependency  = errors.New("wallet verification dependency unavailable")
	ErrWalletSignature   = errors.New("wallet signature rejected")
	wrapped6492Suffix    = bytes.Repeat([]byte{0x64, 0x92}, 16)
)

// VerificationBudget is an immutable per-verifier tightening of hard limits.
type VerificationBudget struct {
	MaxSignatureBytes int
	CallGasLimit      uint64
	Timeout           time.Duration
	ProofMaxAge       time.Duration
}

func (b VerificationBudget) normalized() (VerificationBudget, error) {
	if b.MaxSignatureBytes == 0 {
		b.MaxSignatureBytes = MaxWalletSignatureBytes
	}
	if b.CallGasLimit == 0 {
		b.CallGasLimit = WalletCallGasLimit
	}
	if b.Timeout == 0 {
		b.Timeout = WalletVerificationTimeout
	}
	if b.ProofMaxAge == 0 {
		b.ProofMaxAge = MaxWalletProofAge
	}
	if b.MaxSignatureBytes < 0 || b.MaxSignatureBytes > MaxWalletSignatureBytes || b.CallGasLimit > WalletCallGasLimit || b.Timeout < 0 || b.Timeout > WalletVerificationTimeout || b.ProofMaxAge < 0 || b.ProofMaxAge > MaxWalletProofAge {
		return b, ErrWalletFormat
	}
	return b, nil
}

// AccountChain is a server-selected, chain-aware RPC client. Every read in a
// proof uses one concrete safe anchor; it must never substitute latest.
type AccountChain interface {
	ContractCaller
	ChainID(context.Context) (*big.Int, error)
	HeaderByNumber(context.Context, *big.Int) (*types.Header, error)
	CodeAt(context.Context, common.Address, *big.Int) ([]byte, error)
}

// AccountProof is the observation used to admit or renew a wallet session.
// It is not a bearer credential. Signature bytes stay out of JSON/log output.
type AccountProof struct {
	Address     common.Address `json:"address"`
	ChainID     int64          `json:"chain_id"`
	Kind        string         `json:"kind"`
	Digest      common.Hash    `json:"digest"`
	CodeHash    common.Hash    `json:"code_hash"`
	BlockNumber uint64         `json:"block_number"`
	BlockHash   common.Hash    `json:"block_hash"`
	ObservedAt  time.Time      `json:"observed_at"`
	Signature   []byte         `json:"-"`
}

// AccountVerifier dispatches by on-chain account type before EOA decoding.
// No mutable chain/checker is shared between concurrent verification requests.
type AccountVerifier struct {
	chain   AccountChain
	logger  *zap.Logger
	now     func() time.Time
	budget  VerificationBudget
	initErr error
}

func NewAccountVerifier(chain AccountChain, logger *zap.Logger, budgets ...VerificationBudget) *AccountVerifier {
	if logger == nil {
		logger = zap.NewNop()
	}
	var budget VerificationBudget
	if len(budgets) > 0 {
		budget = budgets[0]
	}
	budget, err := budget.normalized()
	return &AccountVerifier{chain: chain, logger: logger, now: time.Now, budget: budget, initErr: err}
}

// DecodeWalletSignature rejects invalid/oversized hex before allocating or RPC.
func DecodeWalletSignature(encoded string) ([]byte, error) {
	raw := strings.TrimPrefix(encoded, "0x")
	if raw == "" || len(raw)%2 != 0 || len(raw) > 2*MaxWalletSignatureBytes {
		return nil, ErrWalletFormat
	}
	sig, err := hex.DecodeString(raw)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, ErrWalletFormat
	}
	if bytes.HasSuffix(sig, wrapped6492Suffix) {
		return nil, ErrWalletUnsupported
	}
	return sig, nil
}

// Verify validates an admitted message. Challenge, SIWE origin and nonce checks
// belong to the auth service and must run before calling this method.
func (v *AccountVerifier) Verify(ctx context.Context, chainID int64, scheme, address, message, encoded string) (*AccountProof, error) {
	if v.initErr != nil {
		return nil, v.initErr
	}
	if chainID <= 0 || !common.IsHexAddress(address) {
		return nil, ErrWalletFormat
	}
	sig, err := DecodeWalletSignature(encoded)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if len(sig) > v.budget.MaxSignatureBytes {
		return nil, ErrWalletFormat
	}
	if scheme != "siwe" && scheme != "personal_sign" && scheme != "eip712" {
		return nil, ErrWalletUnsupported
	}
	if v.chain == nil {
		return nil, ErrWalletDependency
	}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, v.budget.Timeout)
	defer cancel()
	observedAt := v.now().UTC()
	id, err := v.chain.ChainID(ctx)
	if err != nil || id == nil || id.Cmp(big.NewInt(chainID)) != 0 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: chain identity", ErrWalletDependency)
	}
	anchor, err := v.chain.HeaderByNumber(ctx, big.NewInt(int64(rpc.SafeBlockNumber)))
	if err != nil || anchor == nil || anchor.Number == nil || !anchor.Number.IsUint64() {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: safe anchor", ErrWalletDependency)
	}
	number := new(big.Int).Set(anchor.Number)
	wallet := common.HexToAddress(address)
	code, err := v.chain.CodeAt(ctx, wallet, number)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: account code", ErrWalletDependency)
	}
	// Delegated EOAs are a separate protocol, not ordinary ERC-1271 wallets.
	if bytes.HasPrefix(code, []byte{0xef, 0x01, 0x00}) {
		return nil, ErrWalletUnsupported
	}
	proof := &AccountProof{Address: wallet, ChainID: chainID, Kind: AccountEOA,
		Digest:   crypto.Keccak256Hash([]byte(fmt.Sprintf("\x19Ethereum Signed Message:\n%d%s", len(message), message))),
		CodeHash: crypto.Keccak256Hash(code), BlockNumber: number.Uint64(), BlockHash: anchor.Hash(),
		ObservedAt: observedAt, Signature: append([]byte(nil), sig...)}
	if len(code) != 0 {
		proof.Kind = AccountContract
		if scheme == "eip712" {
			return nil, ErrWalletUnsupported
		}
		valid, callErr := NewEIP1271Checker(v.chain, v.logger).isValidSignatureAt(ctx, address, proof.Digest, sig, number, v.budget.CallGasLimit)
		if callErr != nil {
			return nil, callErr
		}
		if !valid {
			return nil, ErrWalletSignature
		}
	} else {
		digest, verifyErr := v.verifyEOA(ctx, chainID, scheme, address, message, encoded)
		if verifyErr != nil {
			return nil, verifyErr
		}
		proof.Digest = digest
	}
	// Re-read the exact height after all reads (including RPC failover). A
	// number/hash mismatch invalidates the observation instead of reanchoring it.
	recheck, err := v.chain.HeaderByNumber(ctx, number)
	if err != nil || recheck == nil || recheck.Number == nil || recheck.Number.Cmp(number) != 0 || recheck.Hash() != proof.BlockHash {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: anchor changed", ErrWalletDependency)
	}
	if ctx.Err() != nil || v.now().Before(observedAt) || v.now().Sub(observedAt) > v.budget.ProofMaxAge {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: expired observation", ErrWalletDependency)
	}
	return proof, nil
}

func (v *AccountVerifier) verifyEOA(ctx context.Context, chainID int64, scheme, address, message, encoded string) (common.Hash, error) {
	var valid bool
	var err error
	var digest common.Hash
	if scheme == "eip712" {
		var typed EIP712TypedData
		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(message), &typed) != nil || typed.Domain.ChainId == nil || typed.Domain.ChainId.Cmp(big.NewInt(chainID)) != 0 {
			return digest, ErrWalletFormat
		}
		verifier := NewEIP712Verifier(v.logger)
		valid, err = verifier.VerifyTypedData(address, &typed, encoded)
		if err == nil && valid {
			var hash []byte
			hash, err = hashTypedData(verifier.convertToAPITypes(&typed))
			digest = common.BytesToHash(hash)
		}
	} else {
		verifier := NewSignatureVerifier(v.logger)
		valid, err = verifier.VerifySignature(ctx, address, message, encoded)
		digest = common.BytesToHash(verifier.hashMessage(message))
	}
	if err != nil || !valid {
		return digest, ErrWalletSignature
	}
	return digest, nil
}

// Fresh is checked again at issuance after any storage or concurrency wait.
func (p *AccountProof) Fresh(at time.Time) bool {
	return p != nil && !p.ObservedAt.IsZero() && !at.Before(p.ObservedAt) && at.Sub(p.ObservedAt) <= MaxWalletProofAge
}

func isWalletRevert(err error) bool {
	var coded rpc.Error
	return (errors.As(err, &coded) && coded.ErrorCode() == 3) || strings.Contains(strings.ToLower(err.Error()), "execution reverted")
}

func walletCall(ctx context.Context, caller ContractCaller, call ethereum.CallMsg, number *big.Int) ([]byte, error) {
	result, err := caller.CallContract(ctx, call, number)
	if err != nil {
		if isWalletRevert(err) {
			return nil, ErrWalletSignature
		}
		return nil, ErrWalletDependency
	}
	return result, nil
}
